   <a id="utilities-382" name="utilities-382"></a><a href="#utilities-382"><span class="linenos"> 382</span></a>        <span class="n">contour_cp</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">insert</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">contour_cp</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">term_p1</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">midline</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="o">-</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">],</span> <span class="n">axis</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-383" name="utilities-383"></a><a href="#utilities-383"><span class="linenos"> 383</span></a>        <span class="n">contour_cp</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">insert</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">contour_cp</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">term_p2</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">midline</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">],</span> <span class="n">axis</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-384" name="utilities-384"></a><a href="#utilities-384"><span class="linenos"> 384</span></a>

   <a id="utilities-386" name="utilities-386"></a><a href="#utilities-386"><span class="linenos"> 386</span></a>        <span class="k">raise</span> <span class="ne">ValueError</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">"Two endpoints are identical!"</span><span class="p">)</span>

   <a id="utilities-389" name="utilities-389"></a><a href="#utilities-389"><span class="linenos"> 389</span></a>        <span class="c1"># print(id1, id2, id3, id4, pos1, pos2, len(contour_cp))</span>
   <a id="utilities-390" name="utilities-390"></a><a href="#utilities-390"><span class="linenos"> 390</span></a>        <span class="n">half_contour_1</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">contour_cp</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="n">pos1</span> <span class="p">:</span> <span class="nb">min</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">pos2</span> <span class="o">+</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="nb">len</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">contour_cp</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">-</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">)]</span>
   <a id="utilities-391" name="utilities-391"></a><a href="#utilities-391"><span class="linenos"> 391</span></a>        <span class="n">half_contour_2</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">concatenate</span><span class="p">([</span><span class="n">contour_cp</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="n">pos2</span><span class="p">:],</span> <span class="n">contour_cp</span><span class="p">[:</span> <span class="n">pos1</span> <span class="o">+</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">]])</span>

   <a id="utilities-956" name="utilities-956"></a><a href="#utilities-956"><span class="linenos"> 956</span></a>    <span class="n">upper_limit</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mf">0.75</span><span class="p">,</span>
   <a id="utilities-957" name="utilities-957"></a><a href="#utilities-957"><span class="linenos"> 957</span></a>    <span class="n">lower_limit</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mf">0.25</span><span class="p">,</span>
   <a id="utilities-958" name="utilities-958"></a><a href="#utilities-958"><span class="linenos"> 958</span></a>    <span class="n">interp_factor</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">10</span><span class="p">,</span>
   <a id="utilities-959" name="utilities-959"></a><a href="#utilities-959"><span class="linenos"> 959</span></a><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-960" name="utilities-960"></a><a href="#utilities-960"><span class="linenos"> 960</span></a><span class="w">    </span><span class="sd">"""</span>
   <a id="utilities-961" name="utilities-961"></a><a href="#utilities-961"><span class="linenos"> 961</span></a><span class="sd">    Compute constriction degree and return both coarse and fine minima positions.</span>
   <a id="utilities-962" name="utilities-962"></a><a href="#utilities-962"><span class="linenos"> 962</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-963" name="utilities-963"></a><a href="#utilities-963"><span class="linenos"> 963</span></a><span class="sd">    Parameters</span>
   <a id="utilities-964" name="utilities-964"></a><a href="#utilities-964"><span class="linenos"> 964</span></a><span class="sd">    ----------</span>
   <a id="utilities-965" name="utilities-965"></a><a href="#utilities-965"><span class="linenos"> 965</span></a><span class="sd">    intensity : array-like</span>
   <a id="utilities-966" name="utilities-966"></a><a href="#utilities-966"><span class="linenos"> 966</span></a><span class="sd">        1D intensity profile.</span>
   <a id="utilities-967" name="utilities-967"></a><a href="#utilities-967"><span class="linenos"> 967</span></a><span class="sd">    new_step_length : array-like</span>
   <a id="utilities-968" name="utilities-968"></a><a href="#utilities-968"><span class="linenos"> 968</span></a><span class="sd">        Currently unused here, kept for API compatibility.</span>
   <a id="utilities-969" name="utilities-969"></a><a href="#utilities-969"><span class="linenos"> 969</span></a><span class="sd">    upper_limit : float, optional</span>
   <a id="utilities-970" name="utilities-970"></a><a href="#utilities-970"><span class="linenos"> 970</span></a><span class="sd">        Upper bound of region to search, as fraction of profile length.</span>
   <a id="utilities-971" name="utilities-971"></a><a href="#utilities-971"><span class="linenos"> 971</span></a><span class="sd">    lower_limit : float, optional</span>
   <a id="utilities-972" name="utilities-972"></a><a href="#utilities-972"><span class="linenos"> 972</span></a><span class="sd">        Lower bound of region to search, as fraction of profile length.</span>

   <a id="utilities-974" name="utilities-974"></a><a href="#utilities-974"><span class="linenos"> 974</span></a><span class="sd">        Upsampling factor for fine-scale interpolation.</span>
   <a id="utilities-975" name="utilities-975"></a><a href="#utilities-975"><span class="linenos"> 975</span></a>

   <a id="utilities-985" name="utilities-985"></a><a href="#utilities-985"><span class="linenos"> 985</span></a><span class="sd">        Constriction position in original intensity index space.</span>
   <a id="utilities-986" name="utilities-986"></a><a href="#utilities-986"><span class="linenos"> 986</span></a><span class="sd">    ctpos_fine : int or list</span>

   <a id="utilities-1048" name="utilities-1048"></a><a href="#utilities-1048"><span class="linenos">1048</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1049" name="utilities-1049"></a><a href="#utilities-1049"><span class="linenos">1049</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Relative position [0, 1]</span>
   <a id="utilities-1050" name="utilities-1050"></a><a href="#utilities-1050"><span class="linenos">1050</span></a>    <span class="n">ctposr</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="nb">float</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">x_fine</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="n">ctpos_fine</span><span class="p">])</span>
   <a id="utilities-1051" name="utilities-1051"></a><a href="#utilities-1051"><span class="linenos">1051</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1052" name="utilities-1052"></a><a href="#utilities-1052"><span class="linenos">1052</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Map fine position back to original/coarse intensity index</span>
   <a id="utilities-1053" name="utilities-1053"></a><a href="#utilities-1053"><span class="linenos">1053</span></a>    <span class="n">ctpos_coarse</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="nb">int</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nb">round</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">ctposr</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="nb">len</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">intensity</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">-</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">)))</span>
   <a id="utilities-1054" name="utilities-1054"></a><a href="#utilities-1054"><span class="linenos">1054</span></a>    <span class="n">ctpos_coarse</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="nb">max</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="nb">min</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">ctpos_coarse</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="nb">len</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">intensity</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">-</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">))</span>
   <a id="utilities-1055" name="utilities-1055"></a><a href="#utilities-1055"><span class="linenos">1055</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1056" name="utilities-1056"></a><a href="#utilities-1056"><span class="linenos">1056</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">minsize</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ctposr</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">minsizeabs</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ctpos_coarse</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ctpos_fine</span>
   <a id="utilities-1057" name="utilities-1057"></a><a href="#utilities-1057"><span class="linenos">1057</span></a>

   <a id="utilities-1059" name="utilities-1059"></a><a href="#utilities-1059"><span class="linenos">1059</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">interp2d</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">image</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-1060" name="utilities-1060"></a><a href="#utilities-1060"><span class="linenos">1060</span></a>    <span class="n">ff</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">interpolate</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">RectBivariateSpline</span><span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-1061" name="utilities-1061"></a><a href="#utilities-1061"><span class="linenos">1061</span></a>        <span class="nb">range</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">image</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">shape</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">]),</span> <span class="nb">range</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">image</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">shape</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">]),</span> <span class="n">image</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">kx</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ky</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">1</span>
   <a id="utilities-1062" name="utilities-1062"></a><a href="#utilities-1062"><span class="linenos">1062</span></a>    <span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1063" name="utilities-1063"></a><a href="#utilities-1063"><span class="linenos">1063</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">ff</span>
   <a id="utilities-1064" name="utilities-1064"></a><a href="#utilities-1064"><span class="linenos">1064</span></a>

   <a id="utilities-1066" name="utilities-1066"></a><a href="#utilities-1066"><span class="linenos">1066</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">get_image_for_frame</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">df_nr</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">images</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-1067" name="utilities-1067"></a><a href="#utilities-1067"><span class="linenos">1067</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">images</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="n">df_nr</span><span class="p">]</span>
   <a id="utilities-1068" name="utilities-1068"></a><a href="#utilities-1068"><span class="linenos">1068</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1069" name="utilities-1069"></a><a href="#utilities-1069"><span class="linenos">1069</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1070" name="utilities-1070"></a><a href="#utilities-1070"><span class="linenos">1070</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">get_cell_mask</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">midline</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">image</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-1071" name="utilities-1071"></a><a href="#utilities-1071"><span class="linenos">1071</span></a>    <span class="n">cropped_img</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cropped_mask</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cropped_contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">x</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">y</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">crop_image</span><span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-1072" name="utilities-1072"></a><a href="#utilities-1072"><span class="linenos">1072</span></a>        <span class="n">image</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">image</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">contour</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">mask_to_crop</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="kc">None</span>
   <a id="utilities-1073" name="utilities-1073"></a><a href="#utilities-1073"><span class="linenos">1073</span></a>    <span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1074" name="utilities-1074"></a><a href="#utilities-1074"><span class="linenos">1074</span></a>    <span class="n">cropped_midline</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">midline</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">copy</span><span class="p">()</span>
   <a id="utilities-1075" name="utilities-1075"></a><a href="#utilities-1075"><span class="linenos">1075</span></a>    <span class="n">cropped_midline</span> <span class="o">-=</span> <span class="p">[</span><span class="n">x</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">y</span><span class="p">]</span>
   <a id="utilities-1076" name="utilities-1076"></a><a href="#utilities-1076"><span class="linenos">1076</span></a>    <span class="n">mask</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">draw_mask</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">cropped_contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cropped_img</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1077" name="utilities-1077"></a><a href="#utilities-1077"><span class="linenos">1077</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">mask</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cropped_img</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cropped_contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cropped_midline</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">x</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">y</span>
   <a id="utilities-1078" name="utilities-1078"></a><a href="#utilities-1078"><span class="linenos">1078</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1079" name="utilities-1079"></a><a href="#utilities-1079"><span class="linenos">1079</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1080" name="utilities-1080"></a><a href="#utilities-1080"><span class="linenos">1080</span></a><span class="c1"># %% Object detection and mesh creation functions</span>
   <a id="utilities-1081" name="utilities-1081"></a><a href="#utilities-1081"><span class="linenos">1081</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">keep_significant_masks</span><span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-1082" name="utilities-1082"></a><a href="#utilities-1082"><span class="linenos">1082</span></a>    <span class="n">nucleoid_mask</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cropped_mask</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">min_overlap_ratio</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mf">0.01</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">max_external_ratio</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mf">0.1</span>
   <a id="utilities-1083" name="utilities-1083"></a><a href="#utilities-1083"><span class="linenos">1083</span></a><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-1084" name="utilities-1084"></a><a href="#utilities-1084"><span class="linenos">1084</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Find connected components (blobs) in the nucleoid_mask</span>
   <a id="utilities-1085" name="utilities-1085"></a><a href="#utilities-1085"><span class="linenos">1085</span></a>    <span class="n">num_labels</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">labels</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">stats</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">_</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">cv</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">connectedComponentsWithStats</span><span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-1086" name="utilities-1086"></a><a href="#utilities-1086"><span class="linenos">1086</span></a>        <span class="n">nucleoid_mask</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">astype</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">uint8</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1087" name="utilities-1087"></a><a href="#utilities-1087"><span class="linenos">1087</span></a>    <span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1088" name="utilities-1088"></a><a href="#utilities-1088"><span class="linenos">1088</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1089" name="utilities-1089"></a><a href="#utilities-1089"><span class="linenos">1089</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Create a mask to keep blobs with significant overlap</span>
   <a id="utilities-1090" name="utilities-1090"></a><a href="#utilities-1090"><span class="linenos">1090</span></a>    <span class="n">significant_mask</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">zeros_like</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">nucleoid_mask</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">dtype</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">uint8</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1091" name="utilities-1091"></a><a href="#utilities-1091"><span class="linenos">1091</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1092" name="utilities-1092"></a><a href="#utilities-1092"><span class="linenos">1092</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Get the total area of the cropped_mask</span>
   <a id="utilities-1093" name="utilities-1093"></a><a href="#utilities-1093"><span class="linenos">1093</span></a>    <span class="n">total_cropped_mask_area</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">sum</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">cropped_mask</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1094" name="utilities-1094"></a><a href="#utilities-1094"><span class="linenos">1094</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1095" name="utilities-1095"></a><a href="#utilities-1095"><span class="linenos">1095</span></a>    <span class="k">for</span> <span class="n">label</span> <span class="ow">in</span> <span class="nb">range</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">num_labels</span><span class="p">):</span>  <span class="c1"># Skip label 0 as it represents the background</span>
   <a id="utilities-1096" name="utilities-1096"></a><a href="#utilities-1096"><span class="linenos">1096</span></a>        <span class="c1"># Extract the blob region for the current label</span>
   <a id="utilities-1097" name="utilities-1097"></a><a href="#utilities-1097"><span class="linenos">1097</span></a>        <span class="n">blob_mask</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">labels</span> <span class="o">==</span> <span class="n">label</span><span class="p">)</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">astype</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">uint8</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1098" name="utilities-1098"></a><a href="#utilities-1098"><span class="linenos">1098</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1099" name="utilities-1099"></a><a href="#utilities-1099"><span class="linenos">1099</span></a>        <span class="c1"># Calculate the overlap with the cropped_mask</span>
   <a id="utilities-1100" name="utilities-1100"></a><a href="#utilities-1100"><span class="linenos">1100</span></a>        <span class="n">overlap_area</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">sum</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">logical_and</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">blob_mask</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cropped_mask</span><span class="p">))</span>
   <a id="utilities-1101" name="utilities-1101"></a><a href="#utilities-1101"><span class="linenos">1101</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1102" name="utilities-1102"></a><a href="#utilities-1102"><span class="linenos">1102</span></a>        <span class="c1"># Calculate the overlap ratio</span>
   <a id="utilities-1103" name="utilities-1103"></a><a href="#utilities-1103"><span class="linenos">1103</span></a>        <span class="n">overlap_ratio</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">overlap_area</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="n">total_cropped_mask_area</span>
   <a id="utilities-1104" name="utilities-1104"></a><a href="#utilities-1104"><span class="linenos">1104</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1105" name="utilities-1105"></a><a href="#utilities-1105"><span class="linenos">1105</span></a>        <span class="c1"># Calculate the external ratio (portion outside of cropped_mask)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1106" name="utilities-1106"></a><a href="#utilities-1106"><span class="linenos">1106</span></a>        <span class="n">external_area</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">sum</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">logical_and</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">blob_mask</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="o">~</span><span class="n">cropped_mask</span><span class="p">))</span>
   <a id="utilities-1107" name="utilities-1107"></a><a href="#utilities-1107"><span class="linenos">1107</span></a>        <span class="n">external_ratio</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">external_area</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">sum</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">blob_mask</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1108" name="utilities-1108"></a><a href="#utilities-1108"><span class="linenos">1108</span></a>        <span class="c1"># Keep blobs that have a significant overlap with the cropped_mask</span>
   <a id="utilities-1109" name="utilities-1109"></a><a href="#utilities-1109"><span class="linenos">1109</span></a>        <span class="c1"># and don't exceed the maximum external ratio</span>
   <a id="utilities-1110" name="utilities-1110"></a><a href="#utilities-1110"><span class="linenos">1110</span></a>        <span class="k">if</span> <span class="n">overlap_ratio</span> <span class="o">&gt;=</span> <span class="n">min_overlap_ratio</span> <span class="ow">and</span> <span class="n">external_ratio</span> <span class="o">&lt;=</span> <span class="n">max_external_ratio</span><span class="p">:</span>
   <a id="utilities-1111" name="utilities-1111"></a><a href="#utilities-1111"><span class="linenos">1111</span></a>            <span class="n">significant_mask</span> <span class="o">|=</span> <span class="n">blob_mask</span>
   <a id="utilities-1112" name="utilities-1112"></a><a href="#utilities-1112"><span class="linenos">1112</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1113" name="utilities-1113"></a><a href="#utilities-1113"><span class="linenos">1113</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">significant_mask</span>
   <a id="utilities-1114" name="utilities-1114"></a><a href="#utilities-1114"><span class="linenos">1114</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1115" name="utilities-1115"></a><a href="#utilities-1115"><span class="linenos">1115</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1116" name="utilities-1116"></a><a href="#utilities-1116"><span class="linenos">1116</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">get_object_masks</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">object_contours</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">object_midlines</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">object_meshes</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">signal</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-1117" name="utilities-1117"></a><a href="#utilities-1117"><span class="linenos">1117</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1118" name="utilities-1118"></a><a href="#utilities-1118"><span class="linenos">1118</span></a>    <span class="n">masks</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="p">[]</span>
   <a id="utilities-1119" name="utilities-1119"></a><a href="#utilities-1119"><span class="linenos">1119</span></a>    <span class="n">cropped_img</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cropped_mask</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cropped_contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">x</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">y</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">crop_image</span><span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-1120" name="utilities-1120"></a><a href="#utilities-1120"><span class="linenos">1120</span></a>        <span class="n">image</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">signal</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">contour</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">mask_to_crop</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="kc">None</span>
   <a id="utilities-1121" name="utilities-1121"></a><a href="#utilities-1121"><span class="linenos">1121</span></a>    <span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1122" name="utilities-1122"></a><a href="#utilities-1122"><span class="linenos">1122</span></a>    <span class="n">cropped_object_contours</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cropped_object_midlines</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cropped_object_meshes</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-1123" name="utilities-1123"></a><a href="#utilities-1123"><span class="linenos">1123</span></a>        <span class="n">get_cropped_object_data</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">object_contours</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">object_midlines</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">object_meshes</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">x</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">y</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1124" name="utilities-1124"></a><a href="#utilities-1124"><span class="linenos">1124</span></a>    <span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1125" name="utilities-1125"></a><a href="#utilities-1125"><span class="linenos">1125</span></a>    <span class="k">for</span> <span class="n">cropped_object_contour</span> <span class="ow">in</span> <span class="n">cropped_object_contours</span><span class="p">:</span>
   <a id="utilities-1126" name="utilities-1126"></a><a href="#utilities-1126"><span class="linenos">1126</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1127" name="utilities-1127"></a><a href="#utilities-1127"><span class="linenos">1127</span></a>        <span class="n">temp_masks</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">draw_mask</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">cropped_object_contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cropped_img</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1128" name="utilities-1128"></a><a href="#utilities-1128"><span class="linenos">1128</span></a>        <span class="n">masks</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">append</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">temp_masks</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1129" name="utilities-1129"></a><a href="#utilities-1129"><span class="linenos">1129</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1130" name="utilities-1130"></a><a href="#utilities-1130"><span class="linenos">1130</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="p">(</span>

   <a id="utilities-1459" name="utilities-1459"></a><a href="#utilities-1459"><span class="linenos">1459</span></a>    <span class="k">except</span> <span class="ne">Exception</span> <span class="k">as</span> <span class="n">e</span><span class="p">:</span>
   <a id="utilities-1460" name="utilities-1460"></a><a href="#utilities-1460"><span class="linenos">1460</span></a>        <span class="n">bactoscoop_logger</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">debug</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="sa">f</span><span class="s2">"Exception occurred: </span><span class="si">{</span><span class="n">e</span><span class="si">}</span><span class="s2">"</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1461" name="utilities-1461"></a><a href="#utilities-1461"><span class="linenos">1461</span></a>        <span class="k">pass</span>  <span class="c1"># needs logging</span>
   <a id="utilities-1462" name="utilities-1462"></a><a href="#utilities-1462"><span class="linenos">1462</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">result</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">midline</span>
   <a id="utilities-1463" name="utilities-1463"></a><a href="#utilities-1463"><span class="linenos">1463</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1464" name="utilities-1464"></a><a href="#utilities-1464"><span class="linenos">1464</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1465" name="utilities-1465"></a><a href="#utilities-1465"><span class="linenos">1465</span></a><span class="c1"># MOMIA</span>
   <a id="utilities-1466" name="utilities-1466"></a><a href="#utilities-1466"><span class="linenos">1466</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">get_profile_mesh</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">mesh</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">width</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">micron_unit</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-1467" name="utilities-1467"></a><a href="#utilities-1467"><span class="linenos">1467</span></a>    <span class="n">l1</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">mesh</span><span class="p">[:,</span> <span class="p">:</span><span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">]</span>
   <a id="utilities-1468" name="utilities-1468"></a><a href="#utilities-1468"><span class="linenos">1468</span></a>    <span class="n">l2</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">mesh</span><span class="p">[:,</span> <span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">:]</span>
   <a id="utilities-1469" name="utilities-1469"></a><a href="#utilities-1469"><span class="linenos">1469</span></a>    <span class="n">sorted_width</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="nb">sorted</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">width</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">reverse</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="kc">True</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1470" name="utilities-1470"></a><a href="#utilities-1470"><span class="linenos">1470</span></a>    <span class="n">width</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="nb">sum</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">sorted_width</span><span class="p">[:</span> <span class="n">math</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">floor</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nb">len</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">sorted_width</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="mi">3</span><span class="p">)])</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="n">math</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">floor</span><span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-1471" name="utilities-1471"></a><a href="#utilities-1471"><span class="linenos">1471</span></a>        <span class="nb">len</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">sorted_width</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="mi">3</span>
   <a id="utilities-1472" name="utilities-1472"></a><a href="#utilities-1472"><span class="linenos">1472</span></a>    <span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1473" name="utilities-1473"></a><a href="#utilities-1473"><span class="linenos">1473</span></a>    <span class="n">N_width</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="nb">int</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nb">round</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">median</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">width</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="mf">0.05</span><span class="p">))</span>
   <a id="utilities-1474" name="utilities-1474"></a><a href="#utilities-1474"><span class="linenos">1474</span></a>    <span class="n">dl</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">l2</span> <span class="o">-</span> <span class="n">l1</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="n">N_width</span>
   <a id="utilities-1475" name="utilities-1475"></a><a href="#utilities-1475"><span class="linenos">1475</span></a>    <span class="n">mult_mat</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">tile</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">arange</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">N_width</span> <span class="o">+</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">),</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="nb">len</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">l1</span><span class="p">),</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">))</span>
   <a id="utilities-1476" name="utilities-1476"></a><a href="#utilities-1476"><span class="linenos">1476</span></a>    <span class="n">mat_x</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">l1</span><span class="p">[:,</span> <span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">][:,</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">newaxis</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="o">+</span> <span class="n">mult_mat</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">dl</span><span class="p">[:,</span> <span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">][:,</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">newaxis</span><span class="p">]</span>
   <a id="utilities-1477" name="utilities-1477"></a><a href="#utilities-1477"><span class="linenos">1477</span></a>    <span class="n">mat_y</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">l1</span><span class="p">[:,</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">][:,</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">newaxis</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="o">+</span> <span class="n">mult_mat</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">dl</span><span class="p">[:,</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">][:,</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">newaxis</span><span class="p">]</span>
   <a id="utilities-1478" name="utilities-1478"></a><a href="#utilities-1478"><span class="linenos">1478</span></a>    <span class="n">profile_mesh</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">array</span><span class="p">([</span><span class="n">mat_x</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">mat_y</span><span class="p">])</span>
   <a id="utilities-1479" name="utilities-1479"></a><a href="#utilities-1479"><span class="linenos">1479</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">profile_mesh</span>
   <a id="utilities-1480" name="utilities-1480"></a><a href="#utilities-1480"><span class="linenos">1480</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1481" name="utilities-1481"></a><a href="#utilities-1481"><span class="linenos">1481</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1482" name="utilities-1482"></a><a href="#utilities-1482"><span class="linenos">1482</span></a><span class="c1"># MOMIA</span>
   <a id="utilities-1483" name="utilities-1483"></a><a href="#utilities-1483"><span class="linenos">1483</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">expand_contour</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">scale</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-1484" name="utilities-1484"></a><a href="#utilities-1484"><span class="linenos">1484</span></a><span class="w">    </span><span class="sd">"""</span>
   <a id="utilities-1485" name="utilities-1485"></a><a href="#utilities-1485"><span class="linenos">1485</span></a><span class="sd">    enlarges contour</span>
   <a id="utilities-1486" name="utilities-1486"></a><a href="#utilities-1486"><span class="linenos">1486</span></a><span class="sd">    :param contour:</span>
   <a id="utilities-1487" name="utilities-1487"></a><a href="#utilities-1487"><span class="linenos">1487</span></a><span class="sd">    :param scale:</span>
   <a id="utilities-1488" name="utilities-1488"></a><a href="#utilities-1488"><span class="linenos">1488</span></a><span class="sd">    :return:</span>
   <a id="utilities-1489" name="utilities-1489"></a><a href="#utilities-1489"><span class="linenos">1489</span></a><span class="sd">    """</span>
   <a id="utilities-1490" name="utilities-1490"></a><a href="#utilities-1490"><span class="linenos">1490</span></a>    <span class="n">area</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="mf">0.5</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">sum</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">diff</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">contour</span><span class="p">[:,</span> <span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">])</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">contour</span><span class="p">[:</span><span class="o">-</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="o">+</span> <span class="n">contour</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">:,</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">]))</span>
   <a id="utilities-1491" name="utilities-1491"></a><a href="#utilities-1491"><span class="linenos">1491</span></a>    <span class="k">if</span> <span class="n">area</span> <span class="o">&lt;</span> <span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">:</span>
   <a id="utilities-1492" name="utilities-1492"></a><a href="#utilities-1492"><span class="linenos">1492</span></a>        <span class="c1"># If the area is negative, flip the sign of the unit perpendicular vector</span>
   <a id="utilities-1493" name="utilities-1493"></a><a href="#utilities-1493"><span class="linenos">1493</span></a>        <span class="n">dxy</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">unit_perpendicular_vector</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">closed</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="kc">True</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1494" name="utilities-1494"></a><a href="#utilities-1494"><span class="linenos">1494</span></a>    <span class="k">else</span><span class="p">:</span>
   <a id="utilities-1495" name="utilities-1495"></a><a href="#utilities-1495"><span class="linenos">1495</span></a>        <span class="c1"># Otherwise, use the unit perpendicular vector as is</span>
   <a id="utilities-1496" name="utilities-1496"></a><a href="#utilities-1496"><span class="linenos">1496</span></a>        <span class="n">dxy</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">unit_perpendicular_vector</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">closed</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="kc">True</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="o">-</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1497" name="utilities-1497"></a><a href="#utilities-1497"><span class="linenos">1497</span></a>    <span class="c1"># dxy = unit_perpendicular_vector(contour, closed=True)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1498" name="utilities-1498"></a><a href="#utilities-1498"><span class="linenos">1498</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1499" name="utilities-1499"></a><a href="#utilities-1499"><span class="linenos">1499</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">contour</span> <span class="o">-</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">scale</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">dxy</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1500" name="utilities-1500"></a><a href="#utilities-1500"><span class="linenos">1500</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1501" name="utilities-1501"></a><a href="#utilities-1501"><span class="linenos">1501</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1502" name="utilities-1502"></a><a href="#utilities-1502"><span class="linenos">1502</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">erode_contour</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">scale</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-1503" name="utilities-1503"></a><a href="#utilities-1503"><span class="linenos">1503</span></a><span class="w">    </span><span class="sd">"""</span>
   <a id="utilities-1504" name="utilities-1504"></a><a href="#utilities-1504"><span class="linenos">1504</span></a><span class="sd">    shrinks contour</span>
   <a id="utilities-1505" name="utilities-1505"></a><a href="#utilities-1505"><span class="linenos">1505</span></a><span class="sd">    :param contour:</span>
   <a id="utilities-1506" name="utilities-1506"></a><a href="#utilities-1506"><span class="linenos">1506</span></a><span class="sd">    :param scale:</span>
   <a id="utilities-1507" name="utilities-1507"></a><a href="#utilities-1507"><span class="linenos">1507</span></a><span class="sd">    :return:</span>
   <a id="utilities-1508" name="utilities-1508"></a><a href="#utilities-1508"><span class="linenos">1508</span></a><span class="sd">    """</span>
   <a id="utilities-1509" name="utilities-1509"></a><a href="#utilities-1509"><span class="linenos">1509</span></a>    <span class="n">area</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="mf">0.5</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">sum</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">diff</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">contour</span><span class="p">[:,</span> <span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">])</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">contour</span><span class="p">[:</span><span class="o">-</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="o">+</span> <span class="n">contour</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">:,</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">]))</span>
   <a id="utilities-1510" name="utilities-1510"></a><a href="#utilities-1510"><span class="linenos">1510</span></a>    <span class="k">if</span> <span class="n">area</span> <span class="o">&lt;</span> <span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">:</span>
   <a id="utilities-1511" name="utilities-1511"></a><a href="#utilities-1511"><span class="linenos">1511</span></a>        <span class="c1"># If the area is negative, flip the sign of the unit perpendicular vector</span>
   <a id="utilities-1512" name="utilities-1512"></a><a href="#utilities-1512"><span class="linenos">1512</span></a>        <span class="n">dxy</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">unit_perpendicular_vector</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">closed</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="kc">True</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1513" name="utilities-1513"></a><a href="#utilities-1513"><span class="linenos">1513</span></a>    <span class="k">else</span><span class="p">:</span>
   <a id="utilities-1514" name="utilities-1514"></a><a href="#utilities-1514"><span class="linenos">1514</span></a>        <span class="c1"># Otherwise, use the unit perpendicular vector as is</span>
   <a id="utilities-1515" name="utilities-1515"></a><a href="#utilities-1515"><span class="linenos">1515</span></a>        <span class="n">dxy</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">unit_perpendicular_vector</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">closed</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="kc">True</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="o">-</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1516" name="utilities-1516"></a><a href="#utilities-1516"><span class="linenos">1516</span></a>    <span class="c1"># dxy = unit_perpendicular_vector(contour, closed=True)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1517" name="utilities-1517"></a><a href="#utilities-1517"><span class="linenos">1517</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1518" name="utilities-1518"></a><a href="#utilities-1518"><span class="linenos">1518</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">contour</span> <span class="o">+</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">scale</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">dxy</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1519" name="utilities-1519"></a><a href="#utilities-1519"><span class="linenos">1519</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1520" name="utilities-1520"></a><a href="#utilities-1520"><span class="linenos">1520</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1521" name="utilities-1521"></a><a href="#utilities-1521"><span class="linenos">1521</span></a><span class="c1">### ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</span>
   <a id="utilities-1522" name="utilities-1522"></a><a href="#utilities-1522"><span class="linenos">1522</span></a><span class="c1">### Background subtraction</span>
   <a id="utilities-1523" name="utilities-1523"></a><a href="#utilities-1523"><span class="linenos">1523</span></a><span class="c1"># calculates the average pixel value of the background region in an image,</span>
   <a id="utilities-1524" name="utilities-1524"></a><a href="#utilities-1524"><span class="linenos">1524</span></a><span class="c1"># given a binary cell mask.</span>
   <a id="utilities-1525" name="utilities-1525"></a><a href="#utilities-1525"><span class="linenos">1525</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">mean_background</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">image</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cell_mask</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-1526" name="utilities-1526"></a><a href="#utilities-1526"><span class="linenos">1526</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Create a mask for the background region</span>
   <a id="utilities-1527" name="utilities-1527"></a><a href="#utilities-1527"><span class="linenos">1527</span></a>    <span class="n">background_mask</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">logical_not</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">cell_mask</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1528" name="utilities-1528"></a><a href="#utilities-1528"><span class="linenos">1528</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1529" name="utilities-1529"></a><a href="#utilities-1529"><span class="linenos">1529</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Calculate the average pixel value of the background</span>
   <a id="utilities-1530" name="utilities-1530"></a><a href="#utilities-1530"><span class="linenos">1530</span></a>    <span class="c1"># =&gt;indexing the image array with the background_mask, which selects</span>
   <a id="utilities-1531" name="utilities-1531"></a><a href="#utilities-1531"><span class="linenos">1531</span></a>    <span class="c1">#  only the pixel values</span>
   <a id="utilities-1532" name="utilities-1532"></a><a href="#utilities-1532"><span class="linenos">1532</span></a>    <span class="n">bgr</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">mean</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">image</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="n">background_mask</span><span class="p">])</span>
   <a id="utilities-1533" name="utilities-1533"></a><a href="#utilities-1533"><span class="linenos">1533</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">bgr</span>
   <a id="utilities-1534" name="utilities-1534"></a><a href="#utilities-1534"><span class="linenos">1534</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1535" name="utilities-1535"></a><a href="#utilities-1535"><span class="linenos">1535</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1536" name="utilities-1536"></a><a href="#utilities-1536"><span class="linenos">1536</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">median_background</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">image</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cell_mask</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-1537" name="utilities-1537"></a><a href="#utilities-1537"><span class="linenos">1537</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Create a mask for the background region</span>
   <a id="utilities-1538" name="utilities-1538"></a><a href="#utilities-1538"><span class="linenos">1538</span></a>    <span class="n">background_mask</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">logical_not</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">cell_mask</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1539" name="utilities-1539"></a><a href="#utilities-1539"><span class="linenos">1539</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1540" name="utilities-1540"></a><a href="#utilities-1540"><span class="linenos">1540</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Calculate the average pixel value of the background</span>
   <a id="utilities-1541" name="utilities-1541"></a><a href="#utilities-1541"><span class="linenos">1541</span></a>    <span class="c1"># =&gt;indexing the image array with the background_mask, which selects</span>
   <a id="utilities-1542" name="utilities-1542"></a><a href="#utilities-1542"><span class="linenos">1542</span></a>    <span class="c1">#  only the pixel values</span>
   <a id="utilities-1543" name="utilities-1543"></a><a href="#utilities-1543"><span class="linenos">1543</span></a>    <span class="n">bgr</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">median</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">image</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="n">background_mask</span><span class="p">])</span>
   <a id="utilities-1544" name="utilities-1544"></a><a href="#utilities-1544"><span class="linenos">1544</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">bgr</span>
   <a id="utilities-1545" name="utilities-1545"></a><a href="#utilities-1545"><span class="linenos">1545</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1546" name="utilities-1546"></a><a href="#utilities-1546"><span class="linenos">1546</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1547" name="utilities-1547"></a><a href="#utilities-1547"><span class="linenos">1547</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">poly_func</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">coords</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">a</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">b</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">c</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">d</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">e</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">f</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-1548" name="utilities-1548"></a><a href="#utilities-1548"><span class="linenos">1548</span></a>    <span class="n">x</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">y</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">coords</span>
   <a id="utilities-1549" name="utilities-1549"></a><a href="#utilities-1549"><span class="linenos">1549</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">a</span> <span class="o">+</span> <span class="n">b</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">x</span> <span class="o">+</span> <span class="n">c</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">y</span> <span class="o">+</span> <span class="n">d</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">x</span><span class="o">**</span><span class="mi">2</span> <span class="o">+</span> <span class="n">e</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">x</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">y</span> <span class="o">+</span> <span class="n">f</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">y</span><span class="o">**</span><span class="mi">2</span>
   <a id="utilities-1550" name="utilities-1550"></a><a href="#utilities-1550"><span class="linenos">1550</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1551" name="utilities-1551"></a><a href="#utilities-1551"><span class="linenos">1551</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1552" name="utilities-1552"></a><a href="#utilities-1552"><span class="linenos">1552</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">construct_bg_function</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">image</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">seg_mat</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">sampling_interval</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="kc">None</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">plotF</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="kc">False</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-1553" name="utilities-1553"></a><a href="#utilities-1553"><span class="linenos">1553</span></a><span class="w">    </span><span class="sd">"""</span>
   <a id="utilities-1554" name="utilities-1554"></a><a href="#utilities-1554"><span class="linenos">1554</span></a><span class="sd">    Reconstructs the background of an image using 2D polynomial fitting.</span>
   <a id="utilities-1555" name="utilities-1555"></a><a href="#utilities-1555"><span class="linenos">1555</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1556" name="utilities-1556"></a><a href="#utilities-1556"><span class="linenos">1556</span></a><span class="sd">    Parameters:</span>
   <a id="utilities-1557" name="utilities-1557"></a><a href="#utilities-1557"><span class="linenos">1557</span></a><span class="sd">        image (ndarray): Input grayscale image.</span>
   <a id="utilities-1558" name="utilities-1558"></a><a href="#utilities-1558"><span class="linenos">1558</span></a><span class="sd">        seg_mat (ndarray): Binary segmentation mask (non-zero = foreground).</span>
   <a id="utilities-1559" name="utilities-1559"></a><a href="#utilities-1559"><span class="linenos">1559</span></a><span class="sd">        sampling_interval (int): Interval between samples. Default: height // 64.</span>
   <a id="utilities-1560" name="utilities-1560"></a><a href="#utilities-1560"><span class="linenos">1560</span></a><span class="sd">        plotF (bool): Whether to plot the background fit.</span>
   <a id="utilities-1561" name="utilities-1561"></a><a href="#utilities-1561"><span class="linenos">1561</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1562" name="utilities-1562"></a><a href="#utilities-1562"><span class="linenos">1562</span></a><span class="sd">    Returns:</span>
   <a id="utilities-1563" name="utilities-1563"></a><a href="#utilities-1563"><span class="linenos">1563</span></a><span class="sd">        bg_function (function): Background function F(x, y).</span>
   <a id="utilities-1564" name="utilities-1564"></a><a href="#utilities-1564"><span class="linenos">1564</span></a><span class="sd">        bg_matrix (ndarray): Estimated background image.</span>
   <a id="utilities-1565" name="utilities-1565"></a><a href="#utilities-1565"><span class="linenos">1565</span></a><span class="sd">    """</span>
   <a id="utilities-1566" name="utilities-1566"></a><a href="#utilities-1566"><span class="linenos">1566</span></a>    <span class="n">H</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">W</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">image</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">shape</span>
   <a id="utilities-1567" name="utilities-1567"></a><a href="#utilities-1567"><span class="linenos">1567</span></a>    <span class="k">if</span> <span class="n">sampling_interval</span> <span class="ow">is</span> <span class="kc">None</span><span class="p">:</span>
   <a id="utilities-1568" name="utilities-1568"></a><a href="#utilities-1568"><span class="linenos">1568</span></a>        <span class="n">sampling_interval</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="nb">max</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">H</span> <span class="o">//</span> <span class="mi">64</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1569" name="utilities-1569"></a><a href="#utilities-1569"><span class="linenos">1569</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1570" name="utilities-1570"></a><a href="#utilities-1570"><span class="linenos">1570</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Get background pixel coordinates using sparse sampling</span>
   <a id="utilities-1571" name="utilities-1571"></a><a href="#utilities-1571"><span class="linenos">1571</span></a>    <span class="n">ys</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">xs</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">mgrid</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">:</span><span class="n">H</span><span class="p">:</span><span class="n">sampling_interval</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">:</span><span class="n">W</span><span class="p">:</span><span class="n">sampling_interval</span><span class="p">]</span>
   <a id="utilities-1572" name="utilities-1572"></a><a href="#utilities-1572"><span class="linenos">1572</span></a>    <span class="n">xs_flat</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">xs</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">ravel</span><span class="p">()</span>
   <a id="utilities-1573" name="utilities-1573"></a><a href="#utilities-1573"><span class="linenos">1573</span></a>    <span class="n">ys_flat</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">ys</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">ravel</span><span class="p">()</span>
   <a id="utilities-1574" name="utilities-1574"></a><a href="#utilities-1574"><span class="linenos">1574</span></a>    <span class="n">mask_flat</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">seg_mat</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="n">ys_flat</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">xs_flat</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="o">==</span> <span class="mi">0</span>
   <a id="utilities-1575" name="utilities-1575"></a><a href="#utilities-1575"><span class="linenos">1575</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1576" name="utilities-1576"></a><a href="#utilities-1576"><span class="linenos">1576</span></a>    <span class="n">x_bg</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">xs_flat</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="n">mask_flat</span><span class="p">]</span>
   <a id="utilities-1577" name="utilities-1577"></a><a href="#utilities-1577"><span class="linenos">1577</span></a>    <span class="n">y_bg</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">ys_flat</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="n">mask_flat</span><span class="p">]</span>
   <a id="utilities-1578" name="utilities-1578"></a><a href="#utilities-1578"><span class="linenos">1578</span></a>    <span class="n">z_bg</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">image</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="n">y_bg</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">x_bg</span><span class="p">]</span>
   <a id="utilities-1579" name="utilities-1579"></a><a href="#utilities-1579"><span class="linenos">1579</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1580" name="utilities-1580"></a><a href="#utilities-1580"><span class="linenos">1580</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Fit polynomial model to background samples</span>
   <a id="utilities-1581" name="utilities-1581"></a><a href="#utilities-1581"><span class="linenos">1581</span></a>    <span class="n">popt</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">_</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">curve_fit</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">poly_func</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">x_bg</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">y_bg</span><span class="p">),</span> <span class="n">z_bg</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">p0</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">))</span>
   <a id="utilities-1582" name="utilities-1582"></a><a href="#utilities-1582"><span class="linenos">1582</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1583" name="utilities-1583"></a><a href="#utilities-1583"><span class="linenos">1583</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Vectorized background matrix generation</span>
   <a id="utilities-1584" name="utilities-1584"></a><a href="#utilities-1584"><span class="linenos">1584</span></a>    <span class="n">xx</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">yy</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">meshgrid</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">arange</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">W</span><span class="p">),</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">arange</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">H</span><span class="p">))</span>
   <a id="utilities-1585" name="utilities-1585"></a><a href="#utilities-1585"><span class="linenos">1585</span></a>    <span class="n">bg_matrix</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">poly_func</span><span class="p">((</span><span class="n">xx</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">yy</span><span class="p">),</span> <span class="o">*</span><span class="n">popt</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1586" name="utilities-1586"></a><a href="#utilities-1586"><span class="linenos">1586</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1587" name="utilities-1587"></a><a href="#utilities-1587"><span class="linenos">1587</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Optional plotting</span>
   <a id="utilities-1588" name="utilities-1588"></a><a href="#utilities-1588"><span class="linenos">1588</span></a>    <span class="k">if</span> <span class="n">plotF</span><span class="p">:</span>
   <a id="utilities-1589" name="utilities-1589"></a><a href="#utilities-1589"><span class="linenos">1589</span></a>        <span class="n">plot_interval</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="nb">max</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">sampling_interval</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1590" name="utilities-1590"></a><a href="#utilities-1590"><span class="linenos">1590</span></a>        <span class="n">xx_sample</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">yy_sample</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">meshgrid</span><span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-1591" name="utilities-1591"></a><a href="#utilities-1591"><span class="linenos">1591</span></a>            <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">arange</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">W</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">plot_interval</span><span class="p">),</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">arange</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">H</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">plot_interval</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1592" name="utilities-1592"></a><a href="#utilities-1592"><span class="linenos">1592</span></a>        <span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1593" name="utilities-1593"></a><a href="#utilities-1593"><span class="linenos">1593</span></a>        <span class="n">zz_sample</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">poly_func</span><span class="p">((</span><span class="n">xx_sample</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">yy_sample</span><span class="p">),</span> <span class="o">*</span><span class="n">popt</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1594" name="utilities-1594"></a><a href="#utilities-1594"><span class="linenos">1594</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1595" name="utilities-1595"></a><a href="#utilities-1595"><span class="linenos">1595</span></a>        <span class="n">fig</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">plt</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">figure</span><span class="p">()</span>
   <a id="utilities-1596" name="utilities-1596"></a><a href="#utilities-1596"><span class="linenos">1596</span></a>        <span class="n">ax</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">fig</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">add_subplot</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">111</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">projection</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"3d"</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1597" name="utilities-1597"></a><a href="#utilities-1597"><span class="linenos">1597</span></a>        <span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">plot_surface</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">xx_sample</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">yy_sample</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">zz_sample</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">alpha</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mf">0.5</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cmap</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"viridis"</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1598" name="utilities-1598"></a><a href="#utilities-1598"><span class="linenos">1598</span></a>        <span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">scatter</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">x_bg</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">y_bg</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">z_bg</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">color</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"r"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">label</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"Sampled BG"</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1599" name="utilities-1599"></a><a href="#utilities-1599"><span class="linenos">1599</span></a>        <span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">set_xlabel</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">"X"</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1600" name="utilities-1600"></a><a href="#utilities-1600"><span class="linenos">1600</span></a>        <span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">set_ylabel</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">"Y"</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1601" name="utilities-1601"></a><a href="#utilities-1601"><span class="linenos">1601</span></a>        <span class="n">plt</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">legend</span><span class="p">()</span>
   <a id="utilities-1602" name="utilities-1602"></a><a href="#utilities-1602"><span class="linenos">1602</span></a>        <span class="n">plt</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">show</span><span class="p">()</span>
   <a id="utilities-1603" name="utilities-1603"></a><a href="#utilities-1603"><span class="linenos">1603</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1604" name="utilities-1604"></a><a href="#utilities-1604"><span class="linenos">1604</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Return as function and matrix</span>
   <a id="utilities-1605" name="utilities-1605"></a><a href="#utilities-1605"><span class="linenos">1605</span></a>    <span class="n">bg_function</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="k">lambda</span> <span class="n">x</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">y</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="n">poly_func</span><span class="p">((</span><span class="n">x</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">y</span><span class="p">),</span> <span class="o">*</span><span class="n">popt</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-1606" name="utilities-1606"></a><a href="#utilities-1606"><span class="linenos">1606</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">bg_function</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">bg_matrix</span>
   <a id="utilities-1607" name="utilities-1607"></a><a href="#utilities-1607"><span class="linenos">1607</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1608" name="utilities-1608"></a><a href="#utilities-1608"><span class="linenos">1608</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-1609" name="utilities-1609"></a><a href="#utilities-1609"><span class="linenos">1609</span></a><span class="c1">###  -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</span>
   <a id="utilities-1610" name="utilities-1610"></a><a href="#utilities-1610"><span class="linenos">1610</span></a><span class="c1"># %% Morphological features</span>

   <a id="utilities-2089" name="utilities-2089"></a><a href="#utilities-2089"><span class="linenos">2089</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">get_nucleoid_width_variability</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">widths</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2090" name="utilities-2090"></a><a href="#utilities-2090"><span class="linenos">2090</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Because the CV is unitless and usually expressed as a percentage, it is used instead of the SD to compare the spread of data</span>
   <a id="utilities-2091" name="utilities-2091"></a><a href="#utilities-2091"><span class="linenos">2091</span></a>    <span class="c1"># sets that have different units of measurements or have the same units of measurements but differs greatly in magnitude.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2092" name="utilities-2092"></a><a href="#utilities-2092"><span class="linenos">2092</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Ratio between the standard deviation and the mean ( which in some case could be negative)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2093" name="utilities-2093"></a><a href="#utilities-2093"><span class="linenos">2093</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2094" name="utilities-2094"></a><a href="#utilities-2094"><span class="linenos">2094</span></a>    <span class="n">width_var_list</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="p">[]</span>
   <a id="utilities-2095" name="utilities-2095"></a><a href="#utilities-2095"><span class="linenos">2095</span></a>    <span class="k">if</span> <span class="n">widths</span><span class="p">:</span>
   <a id="utilities-2096" name="utilities-2096"></a><a href="#utilities-2096"><span class="linenos">2096</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2097" name="utilities-2097"></a><a href="#utilities-2097"><span class="linenos">2097</span></a>        <span class="k">for</span> <span class="n">width</span> <span class="ow">in</span> <span class="n">widths</span><span class="p">:</span>
   <a id="utilities-2098" name="utilities-2098"></a><a href="#utilities-2098"><span class="linenos">2098</span></a>            <span class="n">width_var</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">std</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">width</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">mean</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">width</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2099" name="utilities-2099"></a><a href="#utilities-2099"><span class="linenos">2099</span></a>            <span class="n">width_var_list</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">append</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">width_var</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2100" name="utilities-2100"></a><a href="#utilities-2100"><span class="linenos">2100</span></a>
