   <a id="utilities-2129" name="utilities-2129"></a><a href="#utilities-2129"><span class="linenos">2129</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">measure_along_objects_midline_interp2d</span><span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-2130" name="utilities-2130"></a><a href="#utilities-2130"><span class="linenos">2130</span></a>    <span class="n">midlines</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">im_interp2d</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">width</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">7</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">subpixel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mf">0.5</span>
   <a id="utilities-2131" name="utilities-2131"></a><a href="#utilities-2131"><span class="linenos">2131</span></a><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2132" name="utilities-2132"></a><a href="#utilities-2132"><span class="linenos">2132</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2133" name="utilities-2133"></a><a href="#utilities-2133"><span class="linenos">2133</span></a>    <span class="n">prf_list</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="p">[]</span>
   <a id="utilities-2134" name="utilities-2134"></a><a href="#utilities-2134"><span class="linenos">2134</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2135" name="utilities-2135"></a><a href="#utilities-2135"><span class="linenos">2135</span></a>    <span class="k">for</span> <span class="n">midline</span> <span class="ow">in</span> <span class="n">midlines</span><span class="p">:</span>
   <a id="utilities-2136" name="utilities-2136"></a><a href="#utilities-2136"><span class="linenos">2136</span></a>        <span class="k">if</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">any</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">midline</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2137" name="utilities-2137"></a><a href="#utilities-2137"><span class="linenos">2137</span></a>            <span class="c1"># Calculate a unit vector perpendicular to the midline</span>
   <a id="utilities-2138" name="utilities-2138"></a><a href="#utilities-2138"><span class="linenos">2138</span></a>            <span class="n">unit_dxy</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">unit_perpendicular_vector</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">midline</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">closed</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="kc">False</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2139" name="utilities-2139"></a><a href="#utilities-2139"><span class="linenos">2139</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2140" name="utilities-2140"></a><a href="#utilities-2140"><span class="linenos">2140</span></a>            <span class="c1"># Calculate a normalized vector for the width and subpixel resolution</span>
   <a id="utilities-2141" name="utilities-2141"></a><a href="#utilities-2141"><span class="linenos">2141</span></a>            <span class="n">width_normalized_dxy</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">unit_dxy</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">subpixel</span>
   <a id="utilities-2142" name="utilities-2142"></a><a href="#utilities-2142"><span class="linenos">2142</span></a>            <span class="c1"># It prepares the vectors for use in generating profiles at varying distances from the midline.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2143" name="utilities-2143"></a><a href="#utilities-2143"><span class="linenos">2143</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2144" name="utilities-2144"></a><a href="#utilities-2144"><span class="linenos">2144</span></a>            <span class="c1"># Interpolate image values along the midline</span>
   <a id="utilities-2145" name="utilities-2145"></a><a href="#utilities-2145"><span class="linenos">2145</span></a>            <span class="n">data</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">im_interp2d</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">ev</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">midline</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">T</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">],</span> <span class="n">midline</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">T</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">])</span>
   <a id="utilities-2146" name="utilities-2146"></a><a href="#utilities-2146"><span class="linenos">2146</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2147" name="utilities-2147"></a><a href="#utilities-2147"><span class="linenos">2147</span></a>            <span class="c1"># Iterate over width steps and calculate profile values</span>
   <a id="utilities-2148" name="utilities-2148"></a><a href="#utilities-2148"><span class="linenos">2148</span></a>            <span class="k">for</span> <span class="n">i</span> <span class="ow">in</span> <span class="nb">range</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">1</span> <span class="o">+</span> <span class="nb">int</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">width</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="mf">0.5</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="n">subpixel</span><span class="p">)):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2149" name="utilities-2149"></a><a href="#utilities-2149"><span class="linenos">2149</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2150" name="utilities-2150"></a><a href="#utilities-2150"><span class="linenos">2150</span></a>                <span class="n">dxy</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">width_normalized_dxy</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">i</span>
   <a id="utilities-2151" name="utilities-2151"></a><a href="#utilities-2151"><span class="linenos">2151</span></a>                <span class="c1"># This vector represents a displacement in the direction perpendicular to the midline</span>
   <a id="utilities-2152" name="utilities-2152"></a><a href="#utilities-2152"><span class="linenos">2152</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2153" name="utilities-2153"></a><a href="#utilities-2153"><span class="linenos">2153</span></a>                <span class="n">v1</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">midline</span> <span class="o">+</span> <span class="n">dxy</span>
   <a id="utilities-2154" name="utilities-2154"></a><a href="#utilities-2154"><span class="linenos">2154</span></a>                <span class="n">v2</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">midline</span> <span class="o">-</span> <span class="n">dxy</span>
   <a id="utilities-2155" name="utilities-2155"></a><a href="#utilities-2155"><span class="linenos">2155</span></a>                <span class="c1"># calculate two points, v1 and v2, located at distances i units away from the midline in opposite directions.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2156" name="utilities-2156"></a><a href="#utilities-2156"><span class="linenos">2156</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2157" name="utilities-2157"></a><a href="#utilities-2157"><span class="linenos">2157</span></a>                <span class="n">p1</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">im_interp2d</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">ev</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">v1</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">T</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">],</span> <span class="n">v1</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">T</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">])</span>
   <a id="utilities-2158" name="utilities-2158"></a><a href="#utilities-2158"><span class="linenos">2158</span></a>                <span class="c1">##calculate a value p1 by evaluating the image data at the coordinates v1</span>
   <a id="utilities-2159" name="utilities-2159"></a><a href="#utilities-2159"><span class="linenos">2159</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2160" name="utilities-2160"></a><a href="#utilities-2160"><span class="linenos">2160</span></a>                <span class="n">p2</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">im_interp2d</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">ev</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">v2</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">T</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">],</span> <span class="n">v2</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">T</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">])</span>
   <a id="utilities-2161" name="utilities-2161"></a><a href="#utilities-2161"><span class="linenos">2161</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2162" name="utilities-2162"></a><a href="#utilities-2162"><span class="linenos">2162</span></a>                <span class="n">data</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">vstack</span><span class="p">([</span><span class="n">p1</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">data</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">p2</span><span class="p">])</span>
   <a id="utilities-2163" name="utilities-2163"></a><a href="#utilities-2163"><span class="linenos">2163</span></a>                <span class="c1"># Stack arrays in sequence vertically (row wise).</span>
   <a id="utilities-2164" name="utilities-2164"></a><a href="#utilities-2164"><span class="linenos">2164</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2165" name="utilities-2165"></a><a href="#utilities-2165"><span class="linenos">2165</span></a>            <span class="c1"># Calculate the average profile</span>
   <a id="utilities-2166" name="utilities-2166"></a><a href="#utilities-2166"><span class="linenos">2166</span></a>            <span class="n">prf</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">average</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">axis</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2167" name="utilities-2167"></a><a href="#utilities-2167"><span class="linenos">2167</span></a>            <span class="n">sigma</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="mi">2</span>  <span class="c1"># standard deviation of Gaussian filter</span>
   <a id="utilities-2168" name="utilities-2168"></a><a href="#utilities-2168"><span class="linenos">2168</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2169" name="utilities-2169"></a><a href="#utilities-2169"><span class="linenos">2169</span></a>            <span class="n">prf</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">gaussian_filter1d</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">prf</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">sigma</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2170" name="utilities-2170"></a><a href="#utilities-2170"><span class="linenos">2170</span></a>            <span class="c1"># A Gaussian Filter is a low pass filter used for reducing noise (high frequency components)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2171" name="utilities-2171"></a><a href="#utilities-2171"><span class="linenos">2171</span></a>            <span class="c1"># and blurring regions of an image.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2172" name="utilities-2172"></a><a href="#utilities-2172"><span class="linenos">2172</span></a>            <span class="n">prf_list</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">append</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">prf</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2173" name="utilities-2173"></a><a href="#utilities-2173"><span class="linenos">2173</span></a>        <span class="k">else</span><span class="p">:</span>

   <a id="utilities-2175" name="utilities-2175"></a><a href="#utilities-2175"><span class="linenos">2175</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">prf_list</span>
   <a id="utilities-2176" name="utilities-2176"></a><a href="#utilities-2176"><span class="linenos">2176</span></a>

   <a id="utilities-2330" name="utilities-2330"></a><a href="#utilities-2330"><span class="linenos">2330</span></a>            <span class="n">dx</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">x1</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">:]</span> <span class="o">+</span> <span class="n">x2</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">:]</span> <span class="o">-</span> <span class="n">x1</span><span class="p">[:</span><span class="o">-</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="o">-</span> <span class="n">x2</span><span class="p">[:</span><span class="o">-</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">]</span>
   <a id="utilities-2331" name="utilities-2331"></a><a href="#utilities-2331"><span class="linenos">2331</span></a>            <span class="c1"># x1[1:] =&gt; start from index 1</span>
   <a id="utilities-2332" name="utilities-2332"></a><a href="#utilities-2332"><span class="linenos">2332</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2333" name="utilities-2333"></a><a href="#utilities-2333"><span class="linenos">2333</span></a>            <span class="n">dy</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">y1</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">:]</span> <span class="o">+</span> <span class="n">y2</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">:]</span> <span class="o">-</span> <span class="n">y1</span><span class="p">[:</span><span class="o">-</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="o">-</span> <span class="n">y2</span><span class="p">[:</span><span class="o">-</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">]</span>
   <a id="utilities-2334" name="utilities-2334"></a><a href="#utilities-2334"><span class="linenos">2334</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2335" name="utilities-2335"></a><a href="#utilities-2335"><span class="linenos">2335</span></a>            <span class="c1"># Calculate the step length for each segment and multiply by pixel size</span>
   <a id="utilities-2336" name="utilities-2336"></a><a href="#utilities-2336"><span class="linenos">2336</span></a>            <span class="n">step_length</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">sqrt</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">dx</span><span class="o">**</span><span class="mi">2</span> <span class="o">+</span> <span class="n">dy</span><span class="o">**</span><span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">px</span>
   <a id="utilities-2337" name="utilities-2337"></a><a href="#utilities-2337"><span class="linenos">2337</span></a>            <span class="n">nuc_step_length_list</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">append</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">step_length</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2338" name="utilities-2338"></a><a href="#utilities-2338"><span class="linenos">2338</span></a>            <span class="n">nuc_length_list</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">append</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">sum</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">step_length</span><span class="p">))</span>
   <a id="utilities-2339" name="utilities-2339"></a><a href="#utilities-2339"><span class="linenos">2339</span></a>        <span class="k">else</span><span class="p">:</span>
   <a id="utilities-2340" name="utilities-2340"></a><a href="#utilities-2340"><span class="linenos">2340</span></a>            <span class="n">nuc_step_length_list</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">append</span><span class="p">([])</span>
   <a id="utilities-2341" name="utilities-2341"></a><a href="#utilities-2341"><span class="linenos">2341</span></a>            <span class="n">nuc_length_list</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">append</span><span class="p">([])</span>
   <a id="utilities-2342" name="utilities-2342"></a><a href="#utilities-2342"><span class="linenos">2342</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">nuc_step_length_list</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">nuc_length_list</span>
   <a id="utilities-2343" name="utilities-2343"></a><a href="#utilities-2343"><span class="linenos">2343</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2344" name="utilities-2344"></a><a href="#utilities-2344"><span class="linenos">2344</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2345" name="utilities-2345"></a><a href="#utilities-2345"><span class="linenos">2345</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">get_object_surface_area</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">width_nos</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">step_lengths</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2346" name="utilities-2346"></a><a href="#utilities-2346"><span class="linenos">2346</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2347" name="utilities-2347"></a><a href="#utilities-2347"><span class="linenos">2347</span></a>    <span class="n">sum_surface_area_list</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="p">[]</span>
   <a id="utilities-2348" name="utilities-2348"></a><a href="#utilities-2348"><span class="linenos">2348</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2349" name="utilities-2349"></a><a href="#utilities-2349"><span class="linenos">2349</span></a>    <span class="k">for</span> <span class="n">width_no</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">step_length</span> <span class="ow">in</span> <span class="nb">zip</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">width_nos</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">step_lengths</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2350" name="utilities-2350"></a><a href="#utilities-2350"><span class="linenos">2350</span></a>        <span class="k">if</span> <span class="ow">not</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">any</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">isnan</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">width_no</span><span class="p">)):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2351" name="utilities-2351"></a><a href="#utilities-2351"><span class="linenos">2351</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2352" name="utilities-2352"></a><a href="#utilities-2352"><span class="linenos">2352</span></a>            <span class="n">widths</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">width_no</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">:]</span>
   <a id="utilities-2353" name="utilities-2353"></a><a href="#utilities-2353"><span class="linenos">2353</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2354" name="utilities-2354"></a><a href="#utilities-2354"><span class="linenos">2354</span></a>            <span class="c1"># Calculate the surface areas for each segment</span>
   <a id="utilities-2355" name="utilities-2355"></a><a href="#utilities-2355"><span class="linenos">2355</span></a>            <span class="n">surface_areas</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="mi">2</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">pi</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">widths</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">step_length</span>
   <a id="utilities-2356" name="utilities-2356"></a><a href="#utilities-2356"><span class="linenos">2356</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2357" name="utilities-2357"></a><a href="#utilities-2357"><span class="linenos">2357</span></a>            <span class="c1"># Calculate the total surface area by summing up individual surface areas</span>
   <a id="utilities-2358" name="utilities-2358"></a><a href="#utilities-2358"><span class="linenos">2358</span></a>            <span class="n">sum_surface_area</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">sum</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">surface_areas</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2359" name="utilities-2359"></a><a href="#utilities-2359"><span class="linenos">2359</span></a>            <span class="n">sum_surface_area_list</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">append</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">sum_surface_area</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2360" name="utilities-2360"></a><a href="#utilities-2360"><span class="linenos">2360</span></a>        <span class="k">else</span><span class="p">:</span>
   <a id="utilities-2361" name="utilities-2361"></a><a href="#utilities-2361"><span class="linenos">2361</span></a>            <span class="n">sum_surface_area_list</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">append</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">nan</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2362" name="utilities-2362"></a><a href="#utilities-2362"><span class="linenos">2362</span></a>        <span class="n">total_surface_area</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-2363" name="utilities-2363"></a><a href="#utilities-2363"><span class="linenos">2363</span></a>            <span class="p">[</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">nan</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="k">if</span> <span class="ow">not</span> <span class="n">sum_surface_area_list</span> <span class="k">else</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">mean</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">sum_surface_area_list</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2364" name="utilities-2364"></a><a href="#utilities-2364"><span class="linenos">2364</span></a>        <span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2365" name="utilities-2365"></a><a href="#utilities-2365"><span class="linenos">2365</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2366" name="utilities-2366"></a><a href="#utilities-2366"><span class="linenos">2366</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">sum_surface_area_list</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">total_surface_area</span>
   <a id="utilities-2367" name="utilities-2367"></a><a href="#utilities-2367"><span class="linenos">2367</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2368" name="utilities-2368"></a><a href="#utilities-2368"><span class="linenos">2368</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2369" name="utilities-2369"></a><a href="#utilities-2369"><span class="linenos">2369</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">get_object_surface_area_over_volume</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">surface_areas</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">volumes</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2370" name="utilities-2370"></a><a href="#utilities-2370"><span class="linenos">2370</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2371" name="utilities-2371"></a><a href="#utilities-2371"><span class="linenos">2371</span></a>    <span class="n">ratio_list</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="p">[]</span>
   <a id="utilities-2372" name="utilities-2372"></a><a href="#utilities-2372"><span class="linenos">2372</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2373" name="utilities-2373"></a><a href="#utilities-2373"><span class="linenos">2373</span></a>    <span class="k">for</span> <span class="n">surface_area</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">volume</span> <span class="ow">in</span> <span class="nb">zip</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">surface_areas</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">volumes</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2374" name="utilities-2374"></a><a href="#utilities-2374"><span class="linenos">2374</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2375" name="utilities-2375"></a><a href="#utilities-2375"><span class="linenos">2375</span></a>        <span class="n">ratio</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">surface_area</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="n">volume</span>
   <a id="utilities-2376" name="utilities-2376"></a><a href="#utilities-2376"><span class="linenos">2376</span></a>        <span class="n">ratio_list</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">append</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">ratio</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2377" name="utilities-2377"></a><a href="#utilities-2377"><span class="linenos">2377</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2378" name="utilities-2378"></a><a href="#utilities-2378"><span class="linenos">2378</span></a>    <span class="n">avg_ratio</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="p">[</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">nan</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="k">if</span> <span class="ow">not</span> <span class="n">ratio_list</span> <span class="k">else</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">mean</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">ratio_list</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2379" name="utilities-2379"></a><a href="#utilities-2379"><span class="linenos">2379</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2380" name="utilities-2380"></a><a href="#utilities-2380"><span class="linenos">2380</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">ratio_list</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">avg_ratio</span>
   <a id="utilities-2381" name="utilities-2381"></a><a href="#utilities-2381"><span class="linenos">2381</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2382" name="utilities-2382"></a><a href="#utilities-2382"><span class="linenos">2382</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2383" name="utilities-2383"></a><a href="#utilities-2383"><span class="linenos">2383</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">object_bending_energy</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">px</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2384" name="utilities-2384"></a><a href="#utilities-2384"><span class="linenos">2384</span></a><span class="w">    </span><span class="sd">"""</span>
   <a id="utilities-2385" name="utilities-2385"></a><a href="#utilities-2385"><span class="linenos">2385</span></a><span class="sd">    Simplification of the formula from https://doi.org/10.1016/j.comgeo.2013.09.001</span>
   <a id="utilities-2386" name="utilities-2386"></a><a href="#utilities-2386"><span class="linenos">2386</span></a><span class="sd">    """</span>
   <a id="utilities-2387" name="utilities-2387"></a><a href="#utilities-2387"><span class="linenos">2387</span></a>    <span class="n">bending_energy_list</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="p">[]</span>
   <a id="utilities-2388" name="utilities-2388"></a><a href="#utilities-2388"><span class="linenos">2388</span></a>    <span class="n">curvatures</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">_</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">_</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">_</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">_</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">_</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">_</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">get_object_curvature_characteristics</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">px</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2389" name="utilities-2389"></a><a href="#utilities-2389"><span class="linenos">2389</span></a>    <span class="k">for</span> <span class="n">line</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">curvature</span> <span class="ow">in</span> <span class="nb">zip</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">curvatures</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2390" name="utilities-2390"></a><a href="#utilities-2390"><span class="linenos">2390</span></a>        <span class="n">BE</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">bending_energy</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">line</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">curvature</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">px</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2391" name="utilities-2391"></a><a href="#utilities-2391"><span class="linenos">2391</span></a>        <span class="n">bending_energy_list</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">append</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">round</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">BE</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">5</span><span class="p">))</span>
   <a id="utilities-2392" name="utilities-2392"></a><a href="#utilities-2392"><span class="linenos">2392</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2393" name="utilities-2393"></a><a href="#utilities-2393"><span class="linenos">2393</span></a>    <span class="n">avg_bending_energy</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-2394" name="utilities-2394"></a><a href="#utilities-2394"><span class="linenos">2394</span></a>        <span class="p">[</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">nan</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="k">if</span> <span class="ow">not</span> <span class="n">bending_energy_list</span> <span class="k">else</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">mean</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">bending_energy_list</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2395" name="utilities-2395"></a><a href="#utilities-2395"><span class="linenos">2395</span></a>    <span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2396" name="utilities-2396"></a><a href="#utilities-2396"><span class="linenos">2396</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">bending_energy_list</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">avg_bending_energy</span>
   <a id="utilities-2397" name="utilities-2397"></a><a href="#utilities-2397"><span class="linenos">2397</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2398" name="utilities-2398"></a><a href="#utilities-2398"><span class="linenos">2398</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2399" name="utilities-2399"></a><a href="#utilities-2399"><span class="linenos">2399</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">bending_energy</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">line</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">curvature</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">px</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2400" name="utilities-2400"></a><a href="#utilities-2400"><span class="linenos">2400</span></a>    <span class="n">length</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">line_length</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">line</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">px</span>
   <a id="utilities-2401" name="utilities-2401"></a><a href="#utilities-2401"><span class="linenos">2401</span></a>    <span class="n">sum_squared_curvature</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">sum</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">curvature</span><span class="o">**</span><span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2402" name="utilities-2402"></a><a href="#utilities-2402"><span class="linenos">2402</span></a>    <span class="n">bending_energy</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">sum_squared_curvature</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="n">length</span>
   <a id="utilities-2403" name="utilities-2403"></a><a href="#utilities-2403"><span class="linenos">2403</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">round</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">bending_energy</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">5</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2404" name="utilities-2404"></a><a href="#utilities-2404"><span class="linenos">2404</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2405" name="utilities-2405"></a><a href="#utilities-2405"><span class="linenos">2405</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2406" name="utilities-2406"></a><a href="#utilities-2406"><span class="linenos">2406</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">midline_midpoint</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">midline</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2407" name="utilities-2407"></a><a href="#utilities-2407"><span class="linenos">2407</span></a>    <span class="n">middle_index</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="nb">len</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">midline</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">//</span> <span class="mi">2</span>
   <a id="utilities-2408" name="utilities-2408"></a><a href="#utilities-2408"><span class="linenos">2408</span></a>    <span class="n">middle_point</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">midline</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="n">middle_index</span><span class="p">]</span>
   <a id="utilities-2409" name="utilities-2409"></a><a href="#utilities-2409"><span class="linenos">2409</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">middle_point</span>
   <a id="utilities-2410" name="utilities-2410"></a><a href="#utilities-2410"><span class="linenos">2410</span></a>

   <a id="utilities-2651" name="utilities-2651"></a><a href="#utilities-2651"><span class="linenos">2651</span></a>        <span class="n">p1</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">im_interp2d</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">ev</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">v1</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">T</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">],</span> <span class="n">v1</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">T</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">])</span>
   <a id="utilities-2652" name="utilities-2652"></a><a href="#utilities-2652"><span class="linenos">2652</span></a>        <span class="n">p2</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">im_interp2d</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">ev</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">v2</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">T</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">],</span> <span class="n">v2</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">T</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">])</span>
   <a id="utilities-2653" name="utilities-2653"></a><a href="#utilities-2653"><span class="linenos">2653</span></a>        <span class="n">data</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">vstack</span><span class="p">([</span><span class="n">p1</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">data</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">p2</span><span class="p">])</span>
   <a id="utilities-2654" name="utilities-2654"></a><a href="#utilities-2654"><span class="linenos">2654</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">data</span>
   <a id="utilities-2655" name="utilities-2655"></a><a href="#utilities-2655"><span class="linenos">2655</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2656" name="utilities-2656"></a><a href="#utilities-2656"><span class="linenos">2656</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2657" name="utilities-2657"></a><a href="#utilities-2657"><span class="linenos">2657</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">normalize_per_cell</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">profile</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2658" name="utilities-2658"></a><a href="#utilities-2658"><span class="linenos">2658</span></a><span class="w">    </span><span class="sd">"""</span>
   <a id="utilities-2659" name="utilities-2659"></a><a href="#utilities-2659"><span class="linenos">2659</span></a><span class="sd">    Normalize the values in a 1D profile using Min-Max scaling.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2660" name="utilities-2660"></a><a href="#utilities-2660"><span class="linenos">2660</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2661" name="utilities-2661"></a><a href="#utilities-2661"><span class="linenos">2661</span></a><span class="sd">    Parameters:</span>
   <a id="utilities-2662" name="utilities-2662"></a><a href="#utilities-2662"><span class="linenos">2662</span></a><span class="sd">        profile (array-like): A 1D array-like object representing the profile.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2663" name="utilities-2663"></a><a href="#utilities-2663"><span class="linenos">2663</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2664" name="utilities-2664"></a><a href="#utilities-2664"><span class="linenos">2664</span></a><span class="sd">    Returns:</span>
   <a id="utilities-2665" name="utilities-2665"></a><a href="#utilities-2665"><span class="linenos">2665</span></a><span class="sd">        normalized_profile (ndarray): Normalized profile.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2666" name="utilities-2666"></a><a href="#utilities-2666"><span class="linenos">2666</span></a><span class="sd">    """</span>
   <a id="utilities-2667" name="utilities-2667"></a><a href="#utilities-2667"><span class="linenos">2667</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Convert the input to a numpy array for ease of manipulation</span>
   <a id="utilities-2668" name="utilities-2668"></a><a href="#utilities-2668"><span class="linenos">2668</span></a>    <span class="n">profile</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">array</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">profile</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2669" name="utilities-2669"></a><a href="#utilities-2669"><span class="linenos">2669</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2670" name="utilities-2670"></a><a href="#utilities-2670"><span class="linenos">2670</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Calculate the minimum and maximum values in the profile</span>
   <a id="utilities-2671" name="utilities-2671"></a><a href="#utilities-2671"><span class="linenos">2671</span></a>    <span class="n">min_val</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">min</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">profile</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2672" name="utilities-2672"></a><a href="#utilities-2672"><span class="linenos">2672</span></a>    <span class="n">max_val</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">max</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">profile</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2673" name="utilities-2673"></a><a href="#utilities-2673"><span class="linenos">2673</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2674" name="utilities-2674"></a><a href="#utilities-2674"><span class="linenos">2674</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Check if min_val equals max_val to avoid division by zero</span>
   <a id="utilities-2675" name="utilities-2675"></a><a href="#utilities-2675"><span class="linenos">2675</span></a>    <span class="k">if</span> <span class="n">min_val</span> <span class="o">==</span> <span class="n">max_val</span><span class="p">:</span>
   <a id="utilities-2676" name="utilities-2676"></a><a href="#utilities-2676"><span class="linenos">2676</span></a>        <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">zeros_like</span><span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-2677" name="utilities-2677"></a><a href="#utilities-2677"><span class="linenos">2677</span></a>            <span class="n">profile</span>
   <a id="utilities-2678" name="utilities-2678"></a><a href="#utilities-2678"><span class="linenos">2678</span></a>        <span class="p">)</span>  <span class="c1"># Return an array of zeros if all values are the same</span>
   <a id="utilities-2679" name="utilities-2679"></a><a href="#utilities-2679"><span class="linenos">2679</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2680" name="utilities-2680"></a><a href="#utilities-2680"><span class="linenos">2680</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Normalize each value to the range [0, 1]</span>
   <a id="utilities-2681" name="utilities-2681"></a><a href="#utilities-2681"><span class="linenos">2681</span></a>    <span class="n">normalized_profile</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">profile</span> <span class="o">-</span> <span class="n">min_val</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">max_val</span> <span class="o">-</span> <span class="n">min_val</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2682" name="utilities-2682"></a><a href="#utilities-2682"><span class="linenos">2682</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2683" name="utilities-2683"></a><a href="#utilities-2683"><span class="linenos">2683</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">normalized_profile</span>
   <a id="utilities-2684" name="utilities-2684"></a><a href="#utilities-2684"><span class="linenos">2684</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2685" name="utilities-2685"></a><a href="#utilities-2685"><span class="linenos">2685</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2686" name="utilities-2686"></a><a href="#utilities-2686"><span class="linenos">2686</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">get_cumulative_step_length_midline</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">midline</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">px</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2687" name="utilities-2687"></a><a href="#utilities-2687"><span class="linenos">2687</span></a><span class="w">    </span><span class="sd">"""</span>
   <a id="utilities-2688" name="utilities-2688"></a><a href="#utilities-2688"><span class="linenos">2688</span></a><span class="sd">    Calculate cumulative step lengths for each segment in the midline.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2689" name="utilities-2689"></a><a href="#utilities-2689"><span class="linenos">2689</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2690" name="utilities-2690"></a><a href="#utilities-2690"><span class="linenos">2690</span></a><span class="sd">    Parameters:</span>
   <a id="utilities-2691" name="utilities-2691"></a><a href="#utilities-2691"><span class="linenos">2691</span></a><span class="sd">        midline (ndarray): An array containing X and Y coordinates of the midline.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2692" name="utilities-2692"></a><a href="#utilities-2692"><span class="linenos">2692</span></a><span class="sd">        px (float): The pixel size.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2693" name="utilities-2693"></a><a href="#utilities-2693"><span class="linenos">2693</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2694" name="utilities-2694"></a><a href="#utilities-2694"><span class="linenos">2694</span></a><span class="sd">    Returns:</span>
   <a id="utilities-2695" name="utilities-2695"></a><a href="#utilities-2695"><span class="linenos">2695</span></a><span class="sd">        cumulative_step_lengths (ndarray): Array containing cumulative step lengths for each segment in the midline.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2696" name="utilities-2696"></a><a href="#utilities-2696"><span class="linenos">2696</span></a><span class="sd">    """</span>
   <a id="utilities-2697" name="utilities-2697"></a><a href="#utilities-2697"><span class="linenos">2697</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Extract X and Y coordinates from the midline</span>
   <a id="utilities-2698" name="utilities-2698"></a><a href="#utilities-2698"><span class="linenos">2698</span></a>    <span class="n">x</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">midline</span><span class="p">[:,</span> <span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">]</span>
   <a id="utilities-2699" name="utilities-2699"></a><a href="#utilities-2699"><span class="linenos">2699</span></a>    <span class="n">y</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">midline</span><span class="p">[:,</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">]</span>
   <a id="utilities-2700" name="utilities-2700"></a><a href="#utilities-2700"><span class="linenos">2700</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2701" name="utilities-2701"></a><a href="#utilities-2701"><span class="linenos">2701</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Compute the step lengths</span>
   <a id="utilities-2702" name="utilities-2702"></a><a href="#utilities-2702"><span class="linenos">2702</span></a>    <span class="n">dx</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">x</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">:]</span> <span class="o">-</span> <span class="n">x</span><span class="p">[:</span><span class="o">-</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">]</span>
   <a id="utilities-2703" name="utilities-2703"></a><a href="#utilities-2703"><span class="linenos">2703</span></a>    <span class="n">dy</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">y</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">:]</span> <span class="o">-</span> <span class="n">y</span><span class="p">[:</span><span class="o">-</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">]</span>
   <a id="utilities-2704" name="utilities-2704"></a><a href="#utilities-2704"><span class="linenos">2704</span></a>    <span class="n">step_lengths</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">sqrt</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">dx</span><span class="o">**</span><span class="mi">2</span> <span class="o">+</span> <span class="n">dy</span><span class="o">**</span><span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">px</span>
   <a id="utilities-2705" name="utilities-2705"></a><a href="#utilities-2705"><span class="linenos">2705</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2706" name="utilities-2706"></a><a href="#utilities-2706"><span class="linenos">2706</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Calculate cumulative step lengths</span>
   <a id="utilities-2707" name="utilities-2707"></a><a href="#utilities-2707"><span class="linenos">2707</span></a>    <span class="n">cumulative_step_lengths</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">zeros_like</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">x</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2708" name="utilities-2708"></a><a href="#utilities-2708"><span class="linenos">2708</span></a>    <span class="n">cumulative_step_lengths</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">:]</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">cumsum</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">step_lengths</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2709" name="utilities-2709"></a><a href="#utilities-2709"><span class="linenos">2709</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2710" name="utilities-2710"></a><a href="#utilities-2710"><span class="linenos">2710</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">cumulative_step_lengths</span>
   <a id="utilities-2711" name="utilities-2711"></a><a href="#utilities-2711"><span class="linenos">2711</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2712" name="utilities-2712"></a><a href="#utilities-2712"><span class="linenos">2712</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2713" name="utilities-2713"></a><a href="#utilities-2713"><span class="linenos">2713</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">linear_interpolation</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">array</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">num_values</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2714" name="utilities-2714"></a><a href="#utilities-2714"><span class="linenos">2714</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Original x-coordinates</span>
   <a id="utilities-2715" name="utilities-2715"></a><a href="#utilities-2715"><span class="linenos">2715</span></a>    <span class="n">x_original</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">arange</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nb">len</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">array</span><span class="p">))</span>
   <a id="utilities-2716" name="utilities-2716"></a><a href="#utilities-2716"><span class="linenos">2716</span></a>    <span class="c1"># New x-coordinates for interpolation</span>
   <a id="utilities-2717" name="utilities-2717"></a><a href="#utilities-2717"><span class="linenos">2717</span></a>    <span class="n">x_new</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">linspace</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="nb">len</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">array</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">-</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">num_values</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2718" name="utilities-2718"></a><a href="#utilities-2718"><span class="linenos">2718</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Perform linear interpolation</span>
   <a id="utilities-2719" name="utilities-2719"></a><a href="#utilities-2719"><span class="linenos">2719</span></a>    <span class="n">interpolated_values</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">interp</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">x_new</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">x_original</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">array</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2720" name="utilities-2720"></a><a href="#utilities-2720"><span class="linenos">2720</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">interpolated_values</span>
   <a id="utilities-2721" name="utilities-2721"></a><a href="#utilities-2721"><span class="linenos">2721</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2722" name="utilities-2722"></a><a href="#utilities-2722"><span class="linenos">2722</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2723" name="utilities-2723"></a><a href="#utilities-2723"><span class="linenos">2723</span></a><span class="c1"># %% Contour shift functions</span>
   <a id="utilities-2724" name="utilities-2724"></a><a href="#utilities-2724"><span class="linenos">2724</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">get_phase_mask</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">phase_cropped_img</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">phase_log_sigma</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mf">0.5</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2725" name="utilities-2725"></a><a href="#utilities-2725"><span class="linenos">2725</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2726" name="utilities-2726"></a><a href="#utilities-2726"><span class="linenos">2726</span></a>    <span class="n">inverted_phase_cropped_image</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">cv</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">bitwise_not</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">phase_cropped_img</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2727" name="utilities-2727"></a><a href="#utilities-2727"><span class="linenos">2727</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2728" name="utilities-2728"></a><a href="#utilities-2728"><span class="linenos">2728</span></a>    <span class="n">log_filtered_img_phase</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">gaussian_laplace</span><span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-2729" name="utilities-2729"></a><a href="#utilities-2729"><span class="linenos">2729</span></a>        <span class="n">inverted_phase_cropped_image</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">sigma</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">phase_log_sigma</span>
   <a id="utilities-2730" name="utilities-2730"></a><a href="#utilities-2730"><span class="linenos">2730</span></a>    <span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2731" name="utilities-2731"></a><a href="#utilities-2731"><span class="linenos">2731</span></a>    <span class="n">threshold_value</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">threshold_otsu</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">log_filtered_img_phase</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2732" name="utilities-2732"></a><a href="#utilities-2732"><span class="linenos">2732</span></a>    <span class="n">cropped_phase_mask</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">log_filtered_img_phase</span> <span class="o">&gt;</span> <span class="n">threshold_value</span>
   <a id="utilities-2733" name="utilities-2733"></a><a href="#utilities-2733"><span class="linenos">2733</span></a>    <span class="n">cropped_phase_mask</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">cropped_phase_mask</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">astype</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">uint8</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2734" name="utilities-2734"></a><a href="#utilities-2734"><span class="linenos">2734</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2735" name="utilities-2735"></a><a href="#utilities-2735"><span class="linenos">2735</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">cropped_phase_mask</span>
   <a id="utilities-2736" name="utilities-2736"></a><a href="#utilities-2736"><span class="linenos">2736</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2737" name="utilities-2737"></a><a href="#utilities-2737"><span class="linenos">2737</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2738" name="utilities-2738"></a><a href="#utilities-2738"><span class="linenos">2738</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">closing_mask</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">cropped_phase_mask</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">closing_level</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2739" name="utilities-2739"></a><a href="#utilities-2739"><span class="linenos">2739</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2740" name="utilities-2740"></a><a href="#utilities-2740"><span class="linenos">2740</span></a>    <span class="n">mask_closed</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">closing</span><span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-2741" name="utilities-2741"></a><a href="#utilities-2741"><span class="linenos">2741</span></a>        <span class="n">cropped_phase_mask</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">footprint</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">ones</span><span class="p">((</span><span class="n">closing_level</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">closing_level</span><span class="p">))</span>
   <a id="utilities-2742" name="utilities-2742"></a><a href="#utilities-2742"><span class="linenos">2742</span></a>    <span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2743" name="utilities-2743"></a><a href="#utilities-2743"><span class="linenos">2743</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2744" name="utilities-2744"></a><a href="#utilities-2744"><span class="linenos">2744</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">mask_closed</span>
   <a id="utilities-2745" name="utilities-2745"></a><a href="#utilities-2745"><span class="linenos">2745</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2746" name="utilities-2746"></a><a href="#utilities-2746"><span class="linenos">2746</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2747" name="utilities-2747"></a><a href="#utilities-2747"><span class="linenos">2747</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">find_centroid_in_largest_mask</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">phase_mask_closed</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">phase_cropped_contour</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2748" name="utilities-2748"></a><a href="#utilities-2748"><span class="linenos">2748</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Find the largest connected component in the phase mask</span>
   <a id="utilities-2749" name="utilities-2749"></a><a href="#utilities-2749"><span class="linenos">2749</span></a>    <span class="n">labeled_mask</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">num_labels</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">label</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">phase_mask_closed</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2750" name="utilities-2750"></a><a href="#utilities-2750"><span class="linenos">2750</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2751" name="utilities-2751"></a><a href="#utilities-2751"><span class="linenos">2751</span></a>    <span class="k">if</span> <span class="n">num_labels</span> <span class="o">&gt;</span> <span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">:</span>
   <a id="utilities-2752" name="utilities-2752"></a><a href="#utilities-2752"><span class="linenos">2752</span></a>        <span class="n">label_sizes</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">ndimage</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">sum</span><span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-2753" name="utilities-2753"></a><a href="#utilities-2753"><span class="linenos">2753</span></a>            <span class="n">phase_mask_closed</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">labeled_mask</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="nb">range</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">num_labels</span> <span class="o">+</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2754" name="utilities-2754"></a><a href="#utilities-2754"><span class="linenos">2754</span></a>        <span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2755" name="utilities-2755"></a><a href="#utilities-2755"><span class="linenos">2755</span></a>        <span class="n">largest_label</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">argmax</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">label_sizes</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">+</span> <span class="mi">1</span>  <span class="c1"># +1 to adjust for 0-based indexing</span>
   <a id="utilities-2756" name="utilities-2756"></a><a href="#utilities-2756"><span class="linenos">2756</span></a>        <span class="n">largest_mask</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">labeled_mask</span> <span class="o">==</span> <span class="n">largest_label</span>
   <a id="utilities-2757" name="utilities-2757"></a><a href="#utilities-2757"><span class="linenos">2757</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2758" name="utilities-2758"></a><a href="#utilities-2758"><span class="linenos">2758</span></a>        <span class="c1"># Get the centroid of the largest mask</span>
   <a id="utilities-2759" name="utilities-2759"></a><a href="#utilities-2759"><span class="linenos">2759</span></a>        <span class="n">phase_centroid</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">get_centroid</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">largest_mask</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2760" name="utilities-2760"></a><a href="#utilities-2760"><span class="linenos">2760</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2761" name="utilities-2761"></a><a href="#utilities-2761"><span class="linenos">2761</span></a>        <span class="c1"># Check if the centroid lies within the cell contour</span>
   <a id="utilities-2762" name="utilities-2762"></a><a href="#utilities-2762"><span class="linenos">2762</span></a>        <span class="n">phase_cropped_contour</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">phase_cropped_contour</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">astype</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">float32</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2763" name="utilities-2763"></a><a href="#utilities-2763"><span class="linenos">2763</span></a>        <span class="k">if</span> <span class="n">cv</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">pointPolygonTest</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">phase_cropped_contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">phase_centroid</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="kc">False</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">&gt;=</span> <span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">:</span>
   <a id="utilities-2764" name="utilities-2764"></a><a href="#utilities-2764"><span class="linenos">2764</span></a>            <span class="k">return</span> <span class="p">[</span><span class="n">phase_centroid</span><span class="p">]</span>
   <a id="utilities-2765" name="utilities-2765"></a><a href="#utilities-2765"><span class="linenos">2765</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2766" name="utilities-2766"></a><a href="#utilities-2766"><span class="linenos">2766</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="p">[]</span>
   <a id="utilities-2767" name="utilities-2767"></a><a href="#utilities-2767"><span class="linenos">2767</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2768" name="utilities-2768"></a><a href="#utilities-2768"><span class="linenos">2768</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2769" name="utilities-2769"></a><a href="#utilities-2769"><span class="linenos">2769</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">align</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">signal_centroid</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">phase_centroid</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cropped_contour</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2770" name="utilities-2770"></a><a href="#utilities-2770"><span class="linenos">2770</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2771" name="utilities-2771"></a><a href="#utilities-2771"><span class="linenos">2771</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Calculate the translation vector</span>
   <a id="utilities-2772" name="utilities-2772"></a><a href="#utilities-2772"><span class="linenos">2772</span></a>    <span class="n">translation_vector</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">array</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">signal_centroid</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">-</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">array</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">phase_centroid</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2773" name="utilities-2773"></a><a href="#utilities-2773"><span class="linenos">2773</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2774" name="utilities-2774"></a><a href="#utilities-2774"><span class="linenos">2774</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Ensure translation_vector is a 1D array</span>
   <a id="utilities-2775" name="utilities-2775"></a><a href="#utilities-2775"><span class="linenos">2775</span></a>    <span class="n">translation_vector</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">translation_vector</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">flatten</span><span class="p">()</span>
   <a id="utilities-2776" name="utilities-2776"></a><a href="#utilities-2776"><span class="linenos">2776</span></a>    <span class="n">aligned_contour</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">cropped_contour</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">copy</span><span class="p">()</span>
   <a id="utilities-2777" name="utilities-2777"></a><a href="#utilities-2777"><span class="linenos">2777</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2778" name="utilities-2778"></a><a href="#utilities-2778"><span class="linenos">2778</span></a>    <span class="c1"># colume of contour or midline is 2</span>
   <a id="utilities-2779" name="utilities-2779"></a><a href="#utilities-2779"><span class="linenos">2779</span></a>    <span class="k">if</span> <span class="n">aligned_contour</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">shape</span> <span class="o">==</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="nb">len</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">aligned_contour</span><span class="p">),</span> <span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2780" name="utilities-2780"></a><a href="#utilities-2780"><span class="linenos">2780</span></a>        <span class="n">aligned_contour</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">aligned_contour</span> <span class="o">+</span> <span class="n">translation_vector</span>
   <a id="utilities-2781" name="utilities-2781"></a><a href="#utilities-2781"><span class="linenos">2781</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2782" name="utilities-2782"></a><a href="#utilities-2782"><span class="linenos">2782</span></a>    <span class="c1"># colume of mesh is 4</span>
   <a id="utilities-2783" name="utilities-2783"></a><a href="#utilities-2783"><span class="linenos">2783</span></a>    <span class="k">if</span> <span class="n">aligned_contour</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">shape</span> <span class="o">==</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="nb">len</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">aligned_contour</span><span class="p">),</span> <span class="mi">4</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2784" name="utilities-2784"></a><a href="#utilities-2784"><span class="linenos">2784</span></a>        <span class="c1"># Add translation vector to the first and second columns</span>
   <a id="utilities-2785" name="utilities-2785"></a><a href="#utilities-2785"><span class="linenos">2785</span></a>        <span class="n">aligned_contour</span><span class="p">[:,</span> <span class="p">:</span><span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="o">+=</span> <span class="n">translation_vector</span>

   <a id="utilities-2787" name="utilities-2787"></a><a href="#utilities-2787"><span class="linenos">2787</span></a>        <span class="c1"># Add translation vector to the third and fourth columns</span>
   <a id="utilities-2788" name="utilities-2788"></a><a href="#utilities-2788"><span class="linenos">2788</span></a>        <span class="n">aligned_contour</span><span class="p">[:,</span> <span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">:]</span> <span class="o">+=</span> <span class="n">translation_vector</span>

   <a id="utilities-2790" name="utilities-2790"></a><a href="#utilities-2790"><span class="linenos">2790</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">aligned_contour</span>
   <a id="utilities-2791" name="utilities-2791"></a><a href="#utilities-2791"><span class="linenos">2791</span></a>

   <a id="utilities-2809" name="utilities-2809"></a><a href="#utilities-2809"><span class="linenos">2809</span></a>        <span class="n">image</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">signal</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">contour</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">mask_to_crop</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="kc">None</span>

   <a id="utilities-2814" name="utilities-2814"></a><a href="#utilities-2814"><span class="linenos">2814</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Apply Laplacian of Gaussian (LoG) filter to the cropped_img</span>

   <a id="utilities-2820" name="utilities-2820"></a><a href="#utilities-2820"><span class="linenos">2820</span></a>
