   <a id="utilities-2836" name="utilities-2836"></a><a href="#utilities-2836"><span class="linenos">2836</span></a>    <span class="n">min_overlap_ratio</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mf">0.3</span><span class="p">,</span>
   <a id="utilities-2837" name="utilities-2837"></a><a href="#utilities-2837"><span class="linenos">2837</span></a>    <span class="n">max_external_ratio</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mf">0.8</span><span class="p">,</span>
   <a id="utilities-2838" name="utilities-2838"></a><a href="#utilities-2838"><span class="linenos">2838</span></a>    <span class="n">phase_log_sigma</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mf">0.5</span><span class="p">,</span>
   <a id="utilities-2839" name="utilities-2839"></a><a href="#utilities-2839"><span class="linenos">2839</span></a>    <span class="n">phase_closing_level</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">,</span>
   <a id="utilities-2840" name="utilities-2840"></a><a href="#utilities-2840"><span class="linenos">2840</span></a>    <span class="n">signal_closing_level</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">12</span><span class="p">,</span>
   <a id="utilities-2841" name="utilities-2841"></a><a href="#utilities-2841"><span class="linenos">2841</span></a>    <span class="n">max_shift_correction</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">10</span><span class="p">,</span>
   <a id="utilities-2842" name="utilities-2842"></a><a href="#utilities-2842"><span class="linenos">2842</span></a><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2843" name="utilities-2843"></a><a href="#utilities-2843"><span class="linenos">2843</span></a><span class="w">    </span><span class="sd">"""</span>
   <a id="utilities-2844" name="utilities-2844"></a><a href="#utilities-2844"><span class="linenos">2844</span></a><span class="sd">    Aligns the contour, mesh, and midline based on the centroids of the phase and signal masks.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2845" name="utilities-2845"></a><a href="#utilities-2845"><span class="linenos">2845</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2846" name="utilities-2846"></a><a href="#utilities-2846"><span class="linenos">2846</span></a><span class="sd">    Parameters:</span>
   <a id="utilities-2847" name="utilities-2847"></a><a href="#utilities-2847"><span class="linenos">2847</span></a><span class="sd">    - phase_image (array): The phase image.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2848" name="utilities-2848"></a><a href="#utilities-2848"><span class="linenos">2848</span></a><span class="sd">    - signal_image (array): The signal image.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2849" name="utilities-2849"></a><a href="#utilities-2849"><span class="linenos">2849</span></a><span class="sd">    - contour (array): The contour data.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2850" name="utilities-2850"></a><a href="#utilities-2850"><span class="linenos">2850</span></a><span class="sd">    - mesh (array): The mesh data.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2851" name="utilities-2851"></a><a href="#utilities-2851"><span class="linenos">2851</span></a><span class="sd">    - midline (array): The midline data.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2852" name="utilities-2852"></a><a href="#utilities-2852"><span class="linenos">2852</span></a><span class="sd">    - log_sigma (float): Sigma for the log filter for the signal mask.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2853" name="utilities-2853"></a><a href="#utilities-2853"><span class="linenos">2853</span></a><span class="sd">    - kernel_width (int): Width of the dilation kernel.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2854" name="utilities-2854"></a><a href="#utilities-2854"><span class="linenos">2854</span></a><span class="sd">    - min_overlap_ratio (float): Minimum overlap ratio for the signal mask.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2855" name="utilities-2855"></a><a href="#utilities-2855"><span class="linenos">2855</span></a><span class="sd">    - max_external_ratio (float): Maximum external ratio for the signal mask.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2856" name="utilities-2856"></a><a href="#utilities-2856"><span class="linenos">2856</span></a><span class="sd">    - phase_log_sigma (float): Sigma for the log filter for the phase mask.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2857" name="utilities-2857"></a><a href="#utilities-2857"><span class="linenos">2857</span></a><span class="sd">    - phase_closing_level (int): Closing level for the phase mask.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2858" name="utilities-2858"></a><a href="#utilities-2858"><span class="linenos">2858</span></a><span class="sd">    - signal_closing_level (int): Closing level for the signal mask.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2859" name="utilities-2859"></a><a href="#utilities-2859"><span class="linenos">2859</span></a><span class="sd">    - max_shift_correction (float): Maximum allowable distance between centroids for correction.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2860" name="utilities-2860"></a><a href="#utilities-2860"><span class="linenos">2860</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2861" name="utilities-2861"></a><a href="#utilities-2861"><span class="linenos">2861</span></a><span class="sd">    Returns:</span>
   <a id="utilities-2862" name="utilities-2862"></a><a href="#utilities-2862"><span class="linenos">2862</span></a><span class="sd">    - aligned_contour (array): The aligned contour data.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2863" name="utilities-2863"></a><a href="#utilities-2863"><span class="linenos">2863</span></a><span class="sd">    - aligned_mesh (array): The aligned mesh data.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2864" name="utilities-2864"></a><a href="#utilities-2864"><span class="linenos">2864</span></a><span class="sd">    - aligned_midline (array): The aligned midline data.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2865" name="utilities-2865"></a><a href="#utilities-2865"><span class="linenos">2865</span></a><span class="sd">    """</span>
   <a id="utilities-2866" name="utilities-2866"></a><a href="#utilities-2866"><span class="linenos">2866</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Phase image</span>
   <a id="utilities-2867" name="utilities-2867"></a><a href="#utilities-2867"><span class="linenos">2867</span></a>    <span class="n">phase_cropped_img</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">_</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">phase_cropped_contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">x</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">y</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">crop_image</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">phase_image</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">contour</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2868" name="utilities-2868"></a><a href="#utilities-2868"><span class="linenos">2868</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2869" name="utilities-2869"></a><a href="#utilities-2869"><span class="linenos">2869</span></a>    <span class="c1"># FM4-64 image</span>
   <a id="utilities-2870" name="utilities-2870"></a><a href="#utilities-2870"><span class="linenos">2870</span></a>    <span class="n">signal_mask</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cropped_signal</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">_</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">_</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">get_signal_mask</span><span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-2871" name="utilities-2871"></a><a href="#utilities-2871"><span class="linenos">2871</span></a>        <span class="n">signal_image</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">log_sigma</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">min_overlap_ratio</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">max_external_ratio</span>
   <a id="utilities-2872" name="utilities-2872"></a><a href="#utilities-2872"><span class="linenos">2872</span></a>    <span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2873" name="utilities-2873"></a><a href="#utilities-2873"><span class="linenos">2873</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2874" name="utilities-2874"></a><a href="#utilities-2874"><span class="linenos">2874</span></a>    <span class="n">cropped_contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cropped_midline</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cropped_mesh</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">get_cropped_cell_data</span><span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-2875" name="utilities-2875"></a><a href="#utilities-2875"><span class="linenos">2875</span></a>        <span class="n">contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">midline</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">mesh</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">x</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">y</span>
   <a id="utilities-2876" name="utilities-2876"></a><a href="#utilities-2876"><span class="linenos">2876</span></a>    <span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2877" name="utilities-2877"></a><a href="#utilities-2877"><span class="linenos">2877</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2878" name="utilities-2878"></a><a href="#utilities-2878"><span class="linenos">2878</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Get phase mask</span>
   <a id="utilities-2879" name="utilities-2879"></a><a href="#utilities-2879"><span class="linenos">2879</span></a>    <span class="n">cropped_phase_mask</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">get_phase_mask</span><span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-2880" name="utilities-2880"></a><a href="#utilities-2880"><span class="linenos">2880</span></a>        <span class="n">phase_cropped_img</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">phase_log_sigma</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">phase_log_sigma</span>
   <a id="utilities-2881" name="utilities-2881"></a><a href="#utilities-2881"><span class="linenos">2881</span></a>    <span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2882" name="utilities-2882"></a><a href="#utilities-2882"><span class="linenos">2882</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2883" name="utilities-2883"></a><a href="#utilities-2883"><span class="linenos">2883</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Fill the hole within mask</span>
   <a id="utilities-2884" name="utilities-2884"></a><a href="#utilities-2884"><span class="linenos">2884</span></a>    <span class="n">phase_mask_closed</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">closing_mask</span><span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-2885" name="utilities-2885"></a><a href="#utilities-2885"><span class="linenos">2885</span></a>        <span class="n">cropped_phase_mask</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">closing_level</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">phase_closing_level</span>
   <a id="utilities-2886" name="utilities-2886"></a><a href="#utilities-2886"><span class="linenos">2886</span></a>    <span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2887" name="utilities-2887"></a><a href="#utilities-2887"><span class="linenos">2887</span></a>    <span class="n">signal_mask_closed</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">closing_mask</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">signal_mask</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">closing_level</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">signal_closing_level</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2888" name="utilities-2888"></a><a href="#utilities-2888"><span class="linenos">2888</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2889" name="utilities-2889"></a><a href="#utilities-2889"><span class="linenos">2889</span></a>    <span class="n">kernel</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">ones</span><span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-2890" name="utilities-2890"></a><a href="#utilities-2890"><span class="linenos">2890</span></a>        <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">kernel_width</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">kernel_width</span><span class="p">),</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">uint8</span>
   <a id="utilities-2891" name="utilities-2891"></a><a href="#utilities-2891"><span class="linenos">2891</span></a>    <span class="p">)</span>  <span class="c1"># You can adjust the kernel size as needed</span>
   <a id="utilities-2892" name="utilities-2892"></a><a href="#utilities-2892"><span class="linenos">2892</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2893" name="utilities-2893"></a><a href="#utilities-2893"><span class="linenos">2893</span></a>    <span class="n">phase_mask_closed</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">cv</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">dilate</span><span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-2894" name="utilities-2894"></a><a href="#utilities-2894"><span class="linenos">2894</span></a>        <span class="n">phase_mask_closed</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">astype</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">uint8</span><span class="p">),</span> <span class="n">kernel</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">iterations</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">2</span>
   <a id="utilities-2895" name="utilities-2895"></a><a href="#utilities-2895"><span class="linenos">2895</span></a>    <span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2896" name="utilities-2896"></a><a href="#utilities-2896"><span class="linenos">2896</span></a>    <span class="n">signal_mask_closed</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">cv</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">dilate</span><span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-2897" name="utilities-2897"></a><a href="#utilities-2897"><span class="linenos">2897</span></a>        <span class="n">signal_mask_closed</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">astype</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">uint8</span><span class="p">),</span> <span class="n">kernel</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">iterations</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">2</span>
   <a id="utilities-2898" name="utilities-2898"></a><a href="#utilities-2898"><span class="linenos">2898</span></a>    <span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2899" name="utilities-2899"></a><a href="#utilities-2899"><span class="linenos">2899</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2900" name="utilities-2900"></a><a href="#utilities-2900"><span class="linenos">2900</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Find largest mask</span>
   <a id="utilities-2901" name="utilities-2901"></a><a href="#utilities-2901"><span class="linenos">2901</span></a>    <span class="n">phase_centroid</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">find_centroid_in_largest_mask</span><span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-2902" name="utilities-2902"></a><a href="#utilities-2902"><span class="linenos">2902</span></a>        <span class="n">phase_mask_closed</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">phase_cropped_contour</span>
   <a id="utilities-2903" name="utilities-2903"></a><a href="#utilities-2903"><span class="linenos">2903</span></a>    <span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2904" name="utilities-2904"></a><a href="#utilities-2904"><span class="linenos">2904</span></a>    <span class="n">signal_centroid</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">get_centroid</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">signal_mask_closed</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2905" name="utilities-2905"></a><a href="#utilities-2905"><span class="linenos">2905</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2906" name="utilities-2906"></a><a href="#utilities-2906"><span class="linenos">2906</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Calculate the distance between the centroids</span>
   <a id="utilities-2907" name="utilities-2907"></a><a href="#utilities-2907"><span class="linenos">2907</span></a>    <span class="n">distance</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">linalg</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">norm</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">array</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">phase_centroid</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">-</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">array</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">signal_centroid</span><span class="p">))</span>
   <a id="utilities-2908" name="utilities-2908"></a><a href="#utilities-2908"><span class="linenos">2908</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2909" name="utilities-2909"></a><a href="#utilities-2909"><span class="linenos">2909</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Check if the distance exceeds the maximum allowable shift correction</span>
   <a id="utilities-2910" name="utilities-2910"></a><a href="#utilities-2910"><span class="linenos">2910</span></a>    <span class="k">if</span> <span class="n">distance</span> <span class="o">&gt;</span> <span class="n">max_shift_correction</span><span class="p">:</span>
   <a id="utilities-2911" name="utilities-2911"></a><a href="#utilities-2911"><span class="linenos">2911</span></a>        <span class="k">return</span> <span class="kc">None</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="kc">None</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="kc">None</span>
   <a id="utilities-2912" name="utilities-2912"></a><a href="#utilities-2912"><span class="linenos">2912</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2913" name="utilities-2913"></a><a href="#utilities-2913"><span class="linenos">2913</span></a>    <span class="n">cropped_aligned_contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cropped_aligned_midline</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cropped_aligned_mesh</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-2914" name="utilities-2914"></a><a href="#utilities-2914"><span class="linenos">2914</span></a>        <span class="n">align_objects</span><span class="p">(</span>

   <a id="utilities-2918" name="utilities-2918"></a><a href="#utilities-2918"><span class="linenos">2918</span></a>            <span class="n">cropped_contour</span><span class="p">,</span>
   <a id="utilities-2919" name="utilities-2919"></a><a href="#utilities-2919"><span class="linenos">2919</span></a>            <span class="n">cropped_midline</span><span class="p">,</span>

   <a id="utilities-2922" name="utilities-2922"></a><a href="#utilities-2922"><span class="linenos">2922</span></a>    <span class="n">aligned_contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">aligned_midline</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">aligned_mesh</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">get_uncropped_cell_data</span><span class="p">(</span>

   <a id="utilities-2929" name="utilities-2929"></a><a href="#utilities-2929"><span class="linenos">2929</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">crop_signal_with_optional_shift</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">image_obj</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">channel</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">shift_signal</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2930" name="utilities-2930"></a><a href="#utilities-2930"><span class="linenos">2930</span></a>    <span class="n">phase_img</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">image_obj</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">image</span> <span class="k">if</span> <span class="n">shift_signal</span> <span class="k">else</span> <span class="kc">None</span>
   <a id="utilities-2931" name="utilities-2931"></a><a href="#utilities-2931"><span class="linenos">2931</span></a>    <span class="n">cropped_signal</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cropped_mask</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cropped_contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">x_offset</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">y_offset</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">crop_image</span><span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-2932" name="utilities-2932"></a><a href="#utilities-2932"><span class="linenos">2932</span></a>        <span class="n">image_obj</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">bg_channels</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="n">channel</span><span class="p">],</span> <span class="n">contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">phase_img</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">phase_img</span>
   <a id="utilities-2933" name="utilities-2933"></a><a href="#utilities-2933"><span class="linenos">2933</span></a>    <span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2934" name="utilities-2934"></a><a href="#utilities-2934"><span class="linenos">2934</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">cropped_signal</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cropped_mask</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cropped_contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">x_offset</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">y_offset</span>
   <a id="utilities-2935" name="utilities-2935"></a><a href="#utilities-2935"><span class="linenos">2935</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2936" name="utilities-2936"></a><a href="#utilities-2936"><span class="linenos">2936</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2937" name="utilities-2937"></a><a href="#utilities-2937"><span class="linenos">2937</span></a><span class="c1"># %% Membrane features</span>
   <a id="utilities-2938" name="utilities-2938"></a><a href="#utilities-2938"><span class="linenos">2938</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2939" name="utilities-2939"></a><a href="#utilities-2939"><span class="linenos">2939</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2940" name="utilities-2940"></a><a href="#utilities-2940"><span class="linenos">2940</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">change_contour_array_length</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">cell_perimeter</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">scale</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mf">0.02</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">smooth</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2941" name="utilities-2941"></a><a href="#utilities-2941"><span class="linenos">2941</span></a>    <span class="n">num</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="nb">int</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">cell_perimeter</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="n">scale</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2942" name="utilities-2942"></a><a href="#utilities-2942"><span class="linenos">2942</span></a>    <span class="n">adjust_eroded_contour</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">spline_approximation</span><span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-2943" name="utilities-2943"></a><a href="#utilities-2943"><span class="linenos">2943</span></a>        <span class="n">contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">n</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">num</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">smooth_factor</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">smooth</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">closed</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="kc">True</span>
   <a id="utilities-2944" name="utilities-2944"></a><a href="#utilities-2944"><span class="linenos">2944</span></a>    <span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2945" name="utilities-2945"></a><a href="#utilities-2945"><span class="linenos">2945</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">adjust_eroded_contour</span>
   <a id="utilities-2946" name="utilities-2946"></a><a href="#utilities-2946"><span class="linenos">2946</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2947" name="utilities-2947"></a><a href="#utilities-2947"><span class="linenos">2947</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">normalized_intensity_by_image</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">profile</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cropped_image</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2948" name="utilities-2948"></a><a href="#utilities-2948"><span class="linenos">2948</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Convert the input to a numpy array for ease of manipulation</span>
   <a id="utilities-2949" name="utilities-2949"></a><a href="#utilities-2949"><span class="linenos">2949</span></a>    <span class="n">prof</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">array</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">profile</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2950" name="utilities-2950"></a><a href="#utilities-2950"><span class="linenos">2950</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2951" name="utilities-2951"></a><a href="#utilities-2951"><span class="linenos">2951</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Flatten the arrays to avoid issues with multidimensional input</span>
   <a id="utilities-2952" name="utilities-2952"></a><a href="#utilities-2952"><span class="linenos">2952</span></a>    <span class="n">prof_flat</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">prof</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">flatten</span><span class="p">()</span>
   <a id="utilities-2953" name="utilities-2953"></a><a href="#utilities-2953"><span class="linenos">2953</span></a>    <span class="n">cropped_image_flat</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">array</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">cropped_image</span><span class="p">)</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">flatten</span><span class="p">()</span>
   <a id="utilities-2954" name="utilities-2954"></a><a href="#utilities-2954"><span class="linenos">2954</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2955" name="utilities-2955"></a><a href="#utilities-2955"><span class="linenos">2955</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Calculate the mean of the lowest 5% values in the profile</span>
   <a id="utilities-2956" name="utilities-2956"></a><a href="#utilities-2956"><span class="linenos">2956</span></a>    <span class="n">min_percentile_value</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">percentile</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">cropped_image_flat</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">5</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2957" name="utilities-2957"></a><a href="#utilities-2957"><span class="linenos">2957</span></a>    <span class="n">max_percentile_value</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">percentile</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">prof_flat</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">95</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2958" name="utilities-2958"></a><a href="#utilities-2958"><span class="linenos">2958</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2959" name="utilities-2959"></a><a href="#utilities-2959"><span class="linenos">2959</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Calculate the mean of the lowest 5% values in the cropped image</span>
   <a id="utilities-2960" name="utilities-2960"></a><a href="#utilities-2960"><span class="linenos">2960</span></a>    <span class="n">min_intensity</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">mean</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">cropped_image_flat</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="n">cropped_image_flat</span> <span class="o">&lt;=</span> <span class="n">min_percentile_value</span><span class="p">])</span>
   <a id="utilities-2961" name="utilities-2961"></a><a href="#utilities-2961"><span class="linenos">2961</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Calculate the mean of the highest 5% values in the profile</span>
   <a id="utilities-2962" name="utilities-2962"></a><a href="#utilities-2962"><span class="linenos">2962</span></a>    <span class="n">max_intensity</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">mean</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">prof_flat</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="n">prof_flat</span> <span class="o">&gt;=</span> <span class="n">max_percentile_value</span><span class="p">])</span>
   <a id="utilities-2963" name="utilities-2963"></a><a href="#utilities-2963"><span class="linenos">2963</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2964" name="utilities-2964"></a><a href="#utilities-2964"><span class="linenos">2964</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Check if min_intensity equals max_intensity to avoid division by zero</span>
   <a id="utilities-2965" name="utilities-2965"></a><a href="#utilities-2965"><span class="linenos">2965</span></a>    <span class="k">if</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">isclose</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">max_intensity</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">min_intensity</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2966" name="utilities-2966"></a><a href="#utilities-2966"><span class="linenos">2966</span></a>        <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">zeros_like</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">prof</span><span class="p">)</span>  <span class="c1"># Return an array of zeros if all values are the same</span>
   <a id="utilities-2967" name="utilities-2967"></a><a href="#utilities-2967"><span class="linenos">2967</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2968" name="utilities-2968"></a><a href="#utilities-2968"><span class="linenos">2968</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Normalize each value to the range [0, 1] using the calculated min and max intensities</span>
   <a id="utilities-2969" name="utilities-2969"></a><a href="#utilities-2969"><span class="linenos">2969</span></a>    <span class="n">normalized_profile</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">prof</span> <span class="o">-</span> <span class="n">min_intensity</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">max_intensity</span> <span class="o">-</span> <span class="n">min_intensity</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2970" name="utilities-2970"></a><a href="#utilities-2970"><span class="linenos">2970</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2971" name="utilities-2971"></a><a href="#utilities-2971"><span class="linenos">2971</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">normalized_profile</span>
   <a id="utilities-2972" name="utilities-2972"></a><a href="#utilities-2972"><span class="linenos">2972</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2973" name="utilities-2973"></a><a href="#utilities-2973"><span class="linenos">2973</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">get_homogeneity</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">normalized_intensity</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2974" name="utilities-2974"></a><a href="#utilities-2974"><span class="linenos">2974</span></a>    <span class="c1"># for normalized_int in normalized_intensity:</span>
   <a id="utilities-2975" name="utilities-2975"></a><a href="#utilities-2975"><span class="linenos">2975</span></a>    <span class="n">gradient</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">gradient</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">normalized_intensity</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2976" name="utilities-2976"></a><a href="#utilities-2976"><span class="linenos">2976</span></a>    <span class="n">homogeneity</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="nb">abs</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">median</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nb">abs</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">gradient</span><span class="p">))</span> <span class="o">-</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">mean</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nb">abs</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">gradient</span><span class="p">)))</span>
   <a id="utilities-2977" name="utilities-2977"></a><a href="#utilities-2977"><span class="linenos">2977</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">homogeneity</span>
   <a id="utilities-2978" name="utilities-2978"></a><a href="#utilities-2978"><span class="linenos">2978</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2979" name="utilities-2979"></a><a href="#utilities-2979"><span class="linenos">2979</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2980" name="utilities-2980"></a><a href="#utilities-2980"><span class="linenos">2980</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">set_bg_zero</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cropped_img</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2981" name="utilities-2981"></a><a href="#utilities-2981"><span class="linenos">2981</span></a>    <span class="n">cell_cropped_mask</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">draw_mask</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">contour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cropped_img</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2982" name="utilities-2982"></a><a href="#utilities-2982"><span class="linenos">2982</span></a>    <span class="n">bg_zero_img</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">cropped_img</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">cell_cropped_mask</span>
   <a id="utilities-2983" name="utilities-2983"></a><a href="#utilities-2983"><span class="linenos">2983</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">bg_zero_img</span>
   <a id="utilities-2984" name="utilities-2984"></a><a href="#utilities-2984"><span class="linenos">2984</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2985" name="utilities-2985"></a><a href="#utilities-2985"><span class="linenos">2985</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2986" name="utilities-2986"></a><a href="#utilities-2986"><span class="linenos">2986</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">rescale_image_255</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">cropped_image</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">max_value</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">255</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-2987" name="utilities-2987"></a><a href="#utilities-2987"><span class="linenos">2987</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2988" name="utilities-2988"></a><a href="#utilities-2988"><span class="linenos">2988</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Find the minimum and maximum intensity values</span>
   <a id="utilities-2989" name="utilities-2989"></a><a href="#utilities-2989"><span class="linenos">2989</span></a>    <span class="n">min_intensity</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">min</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">cropped_image</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2990" name="utilities-2990"></a><a href="#utilities-2990"><span class="linenos">2990</span></a>    <span class="n">max_intensity</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">max</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">cropped_image</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2991" name="utilities-2991"></a><a href="#utilities-2991"><span class="linenos">2991</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2992" name="utilities-2992"></a><a href="#utilities-2992"><span class="linenos">2992</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Scale the intensity values to the range [0, 255]</span>
   <a id="utilities-2993" name="utilities-2993"></a><a href="#utilities-2993"><span class="linenos">2993</span></a>    <span class="n">scaled_image</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-2994" name="utilities-2994"></a><a href="#utilities-2994"><span class="linenos">2994</span></a>        <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">cropped_image</span> <span class="o">-</span> <span class="n">min_intensity</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">max_intensity</span> <span class="o">-</span> <span class="n">min_intensity</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">max_value</span>
   <a id="utilities-2995" name="utilities-2995"></a><a href="#utilities-2995"><span class="linenos">2995</span></a>    <span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2996" name="utilities-2996"></a><a href="#utilities-2996"><span class="linenos">2996</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-2997" name="utilities-2997"></a><a href="#utilities-2997"><span class="linenos">2997</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Float images are not supported by graycomatrix. Convert the image to an unsigned integer type.</span>
   <a id="utilities-2998" name="utilities-2998"></a><a href="#utilities-2998"><span class="linenos">2998</span></a>    <span class="n">scaled_image</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">scaled_image</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">astype</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">uint16</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-2999" name="utilities-2999"></a><a href="#utilities-2999"><span class="linenos">2999</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-3000" name="utilities-3000"></a><a href="#utilities-3000"><span class="linenos">3000</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">scaled_image</span>
   <a id="utilities-3001" name="utilities-3001"></a><a href="#utilities-3001"><span class="linenos">3001</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-3002" name="utilities-3002"></a><a href="#utilities-3002"><span class="linenos">3002</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-3003" name="utilities-3003"></a><a href="#utilities-3003"><span class="linenos">3003</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">create_co_occurrence_matrix</span><span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-3004" name="utilities-3004"></a><a href="#utilities-3004"><span class="linenos">3004</span></a>    <span class="n">cropped_img</span><span class="p">,</span>
   <a id="utilities-3005" name="utilities-3005"></a><a href="#utilities-3005"><span class="linenos">3005</span></a>    <span class="n">distances</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">],</span>
   <a id="utilities-3006" name="utilities-3006"></a><a href="#utilities-3006"><span class="linenos">3006</span></a>    <span class="n">angles</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">pi</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="mi">4</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">pi</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">3</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">pi</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="mi">4</span><span class="p">],</span>
   <a id="utilities-3007" name="utilities-3007"></a><a href="#utilities-3007"><span class="linenos">3007</span></a>    <span class="n">levels</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">256</span><span class="p">,</span>
   <a id="utilities-3008" name="utilities-3008"></a><a href="#utilities-3008"><span class="linenos">3008</span></a>    <span class="n">symmetric</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="kc">True</span><span class="p">,</span>
   <a id="utilities-3009" name="utilities-3009"></a><a href="#utilities-3009"><span class="linenos">3009</span></a>    <span class="n">normed</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="kc">True</span><span class="p">,</span>
   <a id="utilities-3010" name="utilities-3010"></a><a href="#utilities-3010"><span class="linenos">3010</span></a><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-3011" name="utilities-3011"></a><a href="#utilities-3011"><span class="linenos">3011</span></a><span class="w">    </span><span class="sd">"""</span>
   <a id="utilities-3012" name="utilities-3012"></a><a href="#utilities-3012"><span class="linenos">3012</span></a><span class="sd">    Create a co-occurrence matrix from a cropped image.</span>
   <a id="utilities-3013" name="utilities-3013"></a><a href="#utilities-3013"><span class="linenos">3013</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-3014" name="utilities-3014"></a><a href="#utilities-3014"><span class="linenos">3014</span></a><span class="sd">    Parameters:</span>
   <a id="utilities-3015" name="utilities-3015"></a><a href="#utilities-3015"><span class="linenos">3015</span></a><span class="sd">    - cropped_image: The cropped grayscale image.</span>
   <a id="utilities-3016" name="utilities-3016"></a><a href="#utilities-3016"><span class="linenos">3016</span></a><span class="sd">    - distances: List of pixel pair distances.</span>
   <a id="utilities-3017" name="utilities-3017"></a><a href="#utilities-3017"><span class="linenos">3017</span></a><span class="sd">    - angles: List of angles in radians for pixel pairs.</span>
   <a id="utilities-3018" name="utilities-3018"></a><a href="#utilities-3018"><span class="linenos">3018</span></a><span class="sd">    - levels: Number of gray levels (typically 256 for 8-bit images).</span>
   <a id="utilities-3019" name="utilities-3019"></a><a href="#utilities-3019"><span class="linenos">3019</span></a><span class="sd">    - symmetric: If True, the output matrix P[:, :, d, theta] is symmetric.</span>
   <a id="utilities-3020" name="utilities-3020"></a><a href="#utilities-3020"><span class="linenos">3020</span></a><span class="sd">                 This is accomplished by ignoring the order of value pairs, so both (i, j) and (j, i) are accumulated when (i, j) is encountered for a given offset.</span>
   <a id="utilities-3021" name="utilities-3021"></a><a href="#utilities-3021"><span class="linenos">3021</span></a><span class="sd">    - normed: If True, normalize each matrix P[:, :, d, theta] by dividing by the total number of accumulated co-occurrences for the given offset.</span>
   <a id="utilities-3022" name="utilities-3022"></a><a href="#utilities-3022"><span class="linenos">3022</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-3023" name="utilities-3023"></a><a href="#utilities-3023"><span class="linenos">3023</span></a><span class="sd">    Returns:</span>
   <a id="utilities-3024" name="utilities-3024"></a><a href="#utilities-3024"><span class="linenos">3024</span></a><span class="sd">    - co_occurrence_matrix: The co-occurrence matrix.</span>
   <a id="utilities-3025" name="utilities-3025"></a><a href="#utilities-3025"><span class="linenos">3025</span></a><span class="sd">    """</span>
   <a id="utilities-3026" name="utilities-3026"></a><a href="#utilities-3026"><span class="linenos">3026</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Compute the co-occurrence matrix</span>
   <a id="utilities-3027" name="utilities-3027"></a><a href="#utilities-3027"><span class="linenos">3027</span></a>    <span class="n">co_occurrence_matrix</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">graycomatrix</span><span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-3028" name="utilities-3028"></a><a href="#utilities-3028"><span class="linenos">3028</span></a>        <span class="n">cropped_img</span><span class="p">,</span>
   <a id="utilities-3029" name="utilities-3029"></a><a href="#utilities-3029"><span class="linenos">3029</span></a>        <span class="n">distances</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">distances</span><span class="p">,</span>
   <a id="utilities-3030" name="utilities-3030"></a><a href="#utilities-3030"><span class="linenos">3030</span></a>        <span class="n">angles</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">angles</span><span class="p">,</span>
   <a id="utilities-3031" name="utilities-3031"></a><a href="#utilities-3031"><span class="linenos">3031</span></a>        <span class="n">levels</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">levels</span><span class="p">,</span>
   <a id="utilities-3032" name="utilities-3032"></a><a href="#utilities-3032"><span class="linenos">3032</span></a>        <span class="n">symmetric</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="kc">True</span><span class="p">,</span>
   <a id="utilities-3033" name="utilities-3033"></a><a href="#utilities-3033"><span class="linenos">3033</span></a>        <span class="n">normed</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="kc">True</span><span class="p">,</span>
   <a id="utilities-3034" name="utilities-3034"></a><a href="#utilities-3034"><span class="linenos">3034</span></a>    <span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-3035" name="utilities-3035"></a><a href="#utilities-3035"><span class="linenos">3035</span></a>    <span class="n">co_occurrence_matrix</span><span class="p">[:,</span> <span class="p">:,</span> <span class="p">:,</span> <span class="p">:]</span> <span class="o">*=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">where</span><span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-3036" name="utilities-3036"></a><a href="#utilities-3036"><span class="linenos">3036</span></a>        <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">arange</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">levels</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">==</span> <span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">)[:,</span> <span class="kc">None</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="kc">None</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="kc">None</span><span class="p">]</span>
   <a id="utilities-3037" name="utilities-3037"></a><a href="#utilities-3037"><span class="linenos">3037</span></a>        <span class="o">|</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">arange</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">levels</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">==</span> <span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">)[</span><span class="kc">None</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="p">:,</span> <span class="kc">None</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="kc">None</span><span class="p">],</span>
   <a id="utilities-3038" name="utilities-3038"></a><a href="#utilities-3038"><span class="linenos">3038</span></a>        <span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">,</span>
   <a id="utilities-3039" name="utilities-3039"></a><a href="#utilities-3039"><span class="linenos">3039</span></a>        <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">,</span>
   <a id="utilities-3040" name="utilities-3040"></a><a href="#utilities-3040"><span class="linenos">3040</span></a>    <span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-3041" name="utilities-3041"></a><a href="#utilities-3041"><span class="linenos">3041</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-3042" name="utilities-3042"></a><a href="#utilities-3042"><span class="linenos">3042</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">co_occurrence_matrix</span>
   <a id="utilities-3043" name="utilities-3043"></a><a href="#utilities-3043"><span class="linenos">3043</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-3044" name="utilities-3044"></a><a href="#utilities-3044"><span class="linenos">3044</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-3045" name="utilities-3045"></a><a href="#utilities-3045"><span class="linenos">3045</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">get_glcm_feature_means</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">glcm</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-3046" name="utilities-3046"></a><a href="#utilities-3046"><span class="linenos">3046</span></a><span class="w">    </span><span class="sd">"""</span>
   <a id="utilities-3047" name="utilities-3047"></a><a href="#utilities-3047"><span class="linenos">3047</span></a><span class="sd">    Compute the mean Haralick properties for a GLCM in one pass.</span>
   <a id="utilities-3048" name="utilities-3048"></a><a href="#utilities-3048"><span class="linenos">3048</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-3049" name="utilities-3049"></a><a href="#utilities-3049"><span class="linenos">3049</span></a><span class="sd">    The formulas mirror skimage.feature.graycoprops so callers keep the same</span>
   <a id="utilities-3050" name="utilities-3050"></a><a href="#utilities-3050"><span class="linenos">3050</span></a><span class="sd">    numerical behavior while avoiding repeated scans of the same matrix.</span>
   <a id="utilities-3051" name="utilities-3051"></a><a href="#utilities-3051"><span class="linenos">3051</span></a><span class="sd">    """</span>
   <a id="utilities-3052" name="utilities-3052"></a><a href="#utilities-3052"><span class="linenos">3052</span></a>    <span class="k">if</span> <span class="n">glcm</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">ndim</span> <span class="o">!=</span> <span class="mi">4</span><span class="p">:</span>
   <a id="utilities-3053" name="utilities-3053"></a><a href="#utilities-3053"><span class="linenos">3053</span></a>        <span class="k">raise</span> <span class="ne">ValueError</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">"GLCM must be a 4D array."</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-3054" name="utilities-3054"></a><a href="#utilities-3054"><span class="linenos">3054</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-3055" name="utilities-3055"></a><a href="#utilities-3055"><span class="linenos">3055</span></a>    <span class="n">num_level</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">num_level2</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">num_dist</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">num_angle</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">glcm</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">shape</span>
   <a id="utilities-3056" name="utilities-3056"></a><a href="#utilities-3056"><span class="linenos">3056</span></a>    <span class="k">if</span> <span class="n">num_level</span> <span class="o">!=</span> <span class="n">num_level2</span><span class="p">:</span>
   <a id="utilities-3057" name="utilities-3057"></a><a href="#utilities-3057"><span class="linenos">3057</span></a>        <span class="k">raise</span> <span class="ne">ValueError</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">"num_level and num_level2 must be equal."</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-3058" name="utilities-3058"></a><a href="#utilities-3058"><span class="linenos">3058</span></a>    <span class="k">if</span> <span class="n">num_dist</span> <span class="o">&lt;=</span> <span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">:</span>
   <a id="utilities-3059" name="utilities-3059"></a><a href="#utilities-3059"><span class="linenos">3059</span></a>        <span class="k">raise</span> <span class="ne">ValueError</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">"num_dist must be positive."</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-3060" name="utilities-3060"></a><a href="#utilities-3060"><span class="linenos">3060</span></a>    <span class="k">if</span> <span class="n">num_angle</span> <span class="o">&lt;=</span> <span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">:</span>
   <a id="utilities-3061" name="utilities-3061"></a><a href="#utilities-3061"><span class="linenos">3061</span></a>        <span class="k">raise</span> <span class="ne">ValueError</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">"num_angle must be positive."</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-3062" name="utilities-3062"></a><a href="#utilities-3062"><span class="linenos">3062</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-3063" name="utilities-3063"></a><a href="#utilities-3063"><span class="linenos">3063</span></a>    <span class="n">normalized_glcm</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">glcm</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">astype</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">float64</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-3064" name="utilities-3064"></a><a href="#utilities-3064"><span class="linenos">3064</span></a>    <span class="n">glcm_sums</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">sum</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">normalized_glcm</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">axis</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">),</span> <span class="n">keepdims</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="kc">True</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-3065" name="utilities-3065"></a><a href="#utilities-3065"><span class="linenos">3065</span></a>    <span class="n">glcm_sums</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="n">glcm_sums</span> <span class="o">==</span> <span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="mi">1</span>
   <a id="utilities-3066" name="utilities-3066"></a><a href="#utilities-3066"><span class="linenos">3066</span></a>    <span class="n">normalized_glcm</span> <span class="o">/=</span> <span class="n">glcm_sums</span>
   <a id="utilities-3067" name="utilities-3067"></a><a href="#utilities-3067"><span class="linenos">3067</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-3068" name="utilities-3068"></a><a href="#utilities-3068"><span class="linenos">3068</span></a>    <span class="n">I</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">arange</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">num_level</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">dtype</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">float64</span><span class="p">)</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">reshape</span><span class="p">((</span><span class="n">num_level</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">))</span>
   <a id="utilities-3069" name="utilities-3069"></a><a href="#utilities-3069"><span class="linenos">3069</span></a>    <span class="n">J</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">arange</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">num_level</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">dtype</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">float64</span><span class="p">)</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">reshape</span><span class="p">((</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">num_level</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">))</span>
   <a id="utilities-3070" name="utilities-3070"></a><a href="#utilities-3070"><span class="linenos">3070</span></a>    <span class="n">diff</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">I</span> <span class="o">-</span> <span class="n">J</span>
   <a id="utilities-3071" name="utilities-3071"></a><a href="#utilities-3071"><span class="linenos">3071</span></a>    <span class="n">squared_diff</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">diff</span><span class="o">**</span><span class="mi">2</span>
   <a id="utilities-3072" name="utilities-3072"></a><a href="#utilities-3072"><span class="linenos">3072</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-3073" name="utilities-3073"></a><a href="#utilities-3073"><span class="linenos">3073</span></a>    <span class="n">dissimilarity</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">sum</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">normalized_glcm</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">abs</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">diff</span><span class="p">),</span> <span class="n">axis</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">))</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">mean</span><span class="p">()</span>
   <a id="utilities-3074" name="utilities-3074"></a><a href="#utilities-3074"><span class="linenos">3074</span></a>    <span class="n">contrast</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">sum</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">normalized_glcm</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">squared_diff</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">axis</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">))</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">mean</span><span class="p">()</span>
   <a id="utilities-3075" name="utilities-3075"></a><a href="#utilities-3075"><span class="linenos">3075</span></a>    <span class="n">energy</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">sqrt</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">sum</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">normalized_glcm</span><span class="o">**</span><span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">axis</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">)))</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">mean</span><span class="p">()</span>
   <a id="utilities-3076" name="utilities-3076"></a><a href="#utilities-3076"><span class="linenos">3076</span></a>    <span class="n">homogeneity</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">graycoprops</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">glcm</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"homogeneity"</span><span class="p">)</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">mean</span><span class="p">()</span>
   <a id="utilities-3077" name="utilities-3077"></a><a href="#utilities-3077"><span class="linenos">3077</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-3078" name="utilities-3078"></a><a href="#utilities-3078"><span class="linenos">3078</span></a>    <span class="n">diff_i</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">I</span> <span class="o">-</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">sum</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">I</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">normalized_glcm</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">axis</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">))</span>
   <a id="utilities-3079" name="utilities-3079"></a><a href="#utilities-3079"><span class="linenos">3079</span></a>    <span class="n">diff_j</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">J</span> <span class="o">-</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">sum</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">J</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">normalized_glcm</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">axis</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">))</span>
   <a id="utilities-3080" name="utilities-3080"></a><a href="#utilities-3080"><span class="linenos">3080</span></a>    <span class="n">std_i</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">sqrt</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">sum</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">normalized_glcm</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">diff_i</span><span class="o">**</span><span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">),</span> <span class="n">axis</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">)))</span>
   <a id="utilities-3081" name="utilities-3081"></a><a href="#utilities-3081"><span class="linenos">3081</span></a>    <span class="n">std_j</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">sqrt</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">sum</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">normalized_glcm</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">diff_j</span><span class="o">**</span><span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">),</span> <span class="n">axis</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">)))</span>
   <a id="utilities-3082" name="utilities-3082"></a><a href="#utilities-3082"><span class="linenos">3082</span></a>    <span class="n">cov</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">sum</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">normalized_glcm</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">diff_i</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">diff_j</span><span class="p">),</span> <span class="n">axis</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">))</span>
   <a id="utilities-3083" name="utilities-3083"></a><a href="#utilities-3083"><span class="linenos">3083</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-3084" name="utilities-3084"></a><a href="#utilities-3084"><span class="linenos">3084</span></a>    <span class="n">correlation</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">ones</span><span class="p">((</span><span class="n">num_dist</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">num_angle</span><span class="p">),</span> <span class="n">dtype</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">float64</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-3085" name="utilities-3085"></a><a href="#utilities-3085"><span class="linenos">3085</span></a>    <span class="n">non_zero_mask</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">std_i</span> <span class="o">&gt;=</span> <span class="mf">1e-15</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">&amp;</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">std_j</span> <span class="o">&gt;=</span> <span class="mf">1e-15</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-3086" name="utilities-3086"></a><a href="#utilities-3086"><span class="linenos">3086</span></a>    <span class="n">correlation</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="n">non_zero_mask</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="p">(</span>

   <a id="utilities-3176" name="utilities-3176"></a><a href="#utilities-3176"><span class="linenos">3176</span></a>        <span class="n">angles</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">pi</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="mi">4</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">pi</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">3</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">pi</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="mi">4</span><span class="p">],</span>

   <a id="utilities-3184" name="utilities-3184"></a><a href="#utilities-3184"><span class="linenos">3184</span></a><span class="c1"># Function to split the row's array into two halves</span>
   <a id="utilities-3185" name="utilities-3185"></a><a href="#utilities-3185"><span class="linenos">3185</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">split_array</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">row</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-3186" name="utilities-3186"></a><a href="#utilities-3186"><span class="linenos">3186</span></a>    <span class="n">midpoint</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="nb">len</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">row</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">//</span> <span class="mi">2</span>

   <a id="utilities-3188" name="utilities-3188"></a><a href="#utilities-3188"><span class="linenos">3188</span></a>    <span class="n">second_half</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">row</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="n">midpoint</span><span class="p">:]</span>
   <a id="utilities-3189" name="utilities-3189"></a><a href="#utilities-3189"><span class="linenos">3189</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">first_half</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">second_half</span>
   <a id="utilities-3190" name="utilities-3190"></a><a href="#utilities-3190"><span class="linenos">3190</span></a>

   <a id="utilities-3192" name="utilities-3192"></a><a href="#utilities-3192"><span class="linenos">3192</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">get_complemented_contour_intensity</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">row</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-3193" name="utilities-3193"></a><a href="#utilities-3193"><span class="linenos">3193</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-3194" name="utilities-3194"></a><a href="#utilities-3194"><span class="linenos">3194</span></a>    <span class="n">first_half</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">second_half</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">split_array</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">row</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-3195" name="utilities-3195"></a><a href="#utilities-3195"><span class="linenos">3195</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-3196" name="utilities-3196"></a><a href="#utilities-3196"><span class="linenos">3196</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Invert the 'second_half' array</span>
   <a id="utilities-3197" name="utilities-3197"></a><a href="#utilities-3197"><span class="linenos">3197</span></a>    <span class="n">inverted_second_half</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">second_half</span><span class="p">[::</span><span class="o">-</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">]</span>
   <a id="utilities-3198" name="utilities-3198"></a><a href="#utilities-3198"><span class="linenos">3198</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-3199" name="utilities-3199"></a><a href="#utilities-3199"><span class="linenos">3199</span></a>    <span class="c1"># Add corresponding elements from 'first_half' and inverted 'second_half' arrays</span>
   <a id="utilities-3200" name="utilities-3200"></a><a href="#utilities-3200"><span class="linenos">3200</span></a>    <span class="n">complemented_contour_intensity_profile</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">array</span><span class="p">(</span>
   <a id="utilities-3201" name="utilities-3201"></a><a href="#utilities-3201"><span class="linenos">3201</span></a>        <span class="p">[(</span><span class="n">a</span> <span class="o">+</span> <span class="n">b</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="mi">2</span> <span class="k">for</span> <span class="n">a</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">b</span> <span class="ow">in</span> <span class="nb">zip</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">first_half</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">inverted_second_half</span><span class="p">)]</span>

   <a id="utilities-3204" name="utilities-3204"></a><a href="#utilities-3204"><span class="linenos">3204</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">complemented_contour_intensity_profile</span>

   <a id="utilities-3207" name="utilities-3207"></a><a href="#utilities-3207"><span class="linenos">3207</span></a><span class="c1"># %% Shift functions</span>
   <a id="utilities-3208" name="utilities-3208"></a><a href="#utilities-3208"><span class="linenos">3208</span></a><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">shift_image</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">img</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">shift</span><span class="p">):</span>
   <a id="utilities-3209" name="utilities-3209"></a><a href="#utilities-3209"><span class="linenos">3209</span></a><span class="w">    </span><span class="sd">"""</span>
   <a id="utilities-3210" name="utilities-3210"></a><a href="#utilities-3210"><span class="linenos">3210</span></a><span class="sd">    Correct XY drift between phase contrast image and fluorescent image(s).</span>
   <a id="utilities-3211" name="utilities-3211"></a><a href="#utilities-3211"><span class="linenos">3211</span></a><span class="sd">    :param img: Input image</span>
   <a id="utilities-3212" name="utilities-3212"></a><a href="#utilities-3212"><span class="linenos">3212</span></a><span class="sd">    :param shift: Subpixel XY drift</span>
   <a id="utilities-3213" name="utilities-3213"></a><a href="#utilities-3213"><span class="linenos">3213</span></a><span class="sd">    :return: Drift corrected image</span>
   <a id="utilities-3214" name="utilities-3214"></a><a href="#utilities-3214"><span class="linenos">3214</span></a><span class="sd">    """</span>
   <a id="utilities-3215" name="utilities-3215"></a><a href="#utilities-3215"><span class="linenos">3215</span></a>    <span class="n">offset_image</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">fourier_shift</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">fft</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">fftn</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">img</span><span class="p">),</span> <span class="n">shift</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-3216" name="utilities-3216"></a><a href="#utilities-3216"><span class="linenos">3216</span></a>    <span class="n">offset_image</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">fft</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">ifftn</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">offset_image</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-3217" name="utilities-3217"></a><a href="#utilities-3217"><span class="linenos">3217</span></a>    <span class="n">offset_image</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">round</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">offset_image</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">real</span><span class="p">)</span>
   <a id="utilities-3218" name="utilities-3218"></a><a href="#utilities-3218"><span class="linenos">3218</span></a>    <span class="n">offset_image</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="n">offset_image</span> <span class="o">&lt;=</span> <span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="mi">10</span>
   <a id="utilities-3219" name="utilities-3219"></a><a href="#utilities-3219"><span class="linenos">3219</span></a>
   <a id="utilities-3220" name="utilities-3220"></a><a href="#utilities-3220"><span class="linenos">3220</span></a>    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">offset_image</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">astype</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">np</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">uint16</span><span class="p">)</span>
